## PRE-FLIGHT (execute ALL in parallel before reading anything)

```bash
git log --oneline -7                        # recent history + commit style
git status --short                          # uncommitted changes
git diff HEAD --stat                        # change scope
npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | tail -20           # TypeScript strict check
docker ps --filter "name=mcorch" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"
docker ps --filter "name=mega-brain" --format "{{.Names}}: {{.Status}}"
curl -s http://localhost:8001/api/v2/heartbeat  # Chroma API v2 health
ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null                    # BoK suites disponíveis
wc -l HANDOFF.md                            # total lines — drives the read-from-end offset
```

Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

Also check for loose files in scratch/:
```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  if [ "$slug" = "security" ]; then
    required_sec=(00-deepsearch-blueprint 01-brd-security 02-srs-secure-spec 03-sdd-hardening-architecture 04-fmea-security)
    missing=()
    for doc in "${required_sec[@]}"; do
      [ -f "docs/bok/security/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
    done
    [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ security — BoK complete (ciso-4-artifact)" || echo "⚠️ security — missing: ${missing[*]}"
    continue
  fi
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 8985	### Embedding Model
> 8986	`openai/text-embedding-3-small`, `dimensions: 768` (truncated output via OpenRouter).

> 8988	
> 8989	### Embedding Pipeline (Autônomo — 2026-05-03)
> 8990	Três camadas eliminam intervenção manual:
> 8991	1. **INSERT trigger** — `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` (pg_net) dispara `embed-mcorch-node` assincronamente em todo INSERT onde `embedding IS NULL`. Nunca bloqueia o INSERT.
> 8992	2. **Post-commit hook** — `.git/hooks/post-commit` roda `node scripts/ingest-codebase.ts --quiet` em background após cada commit → re-ingesta AST.
> 8993	3. **MeshControlPanel** — UI bottom-left do MatrixPage: "Sync Embeddings" (`trigger-embeddings`) e "Refresh Mesh Stats" (`trigger-ingest`).
> 8994

> 8999	  -H "apikey: $SB_SECRET_KEY" | python3 -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(f'{len(d)} nós sem embedding')"
> 9000	
> 9001	# Backfill manual (Bun-native — requer .env):

> 9003	
> 9004	# Backfill de código-fonte nos nós AST:

> 9006	
> 9007	# Re-sync AST completo:

> 9009	
> 9010	# Acompanhar re-ingesta pós-commit:

> 9119	## Constellation Matrix Intelligence & Stabilization Record (2026-05-03)
> 9120	
> 9121	Sessão de elevação da `ConstellationMatrix` ao nível de **Code Intelligence** e estabilização do motor gráfico. O motor Sigma.js/ForceAtlas2 apresentava loops de renderização infinitos ("blinking") e resets de estado ao interagir com a sidebar. A correção envolveu a estabilização de referências de props, memoização rigorosa das opções do grafo e desacoplamento de filtros dos ciclos de renderização do canvas. Paralelamente, a Matrix foi dotada de inteligência de código nativa via hook `useNodeIntelligence` e um `NodeDetailsPanel` reestruturado com navegação por abas.

# /loop — schedule a recurring or self-paced prompt

Parse the input below into `[interval] <prompt…>` and schedule it.

## Parsing (in priority order)

1. **Leading token**: if the first whitespace-delimited token matches `^\d+[smhd]$` (e.g. `5m`, `2h`), that's the interval; the rest is the prompt.
2. **Trailing "every" clause**: otherwise, if the input ends with `every <N><unit>` or `every <N> <unit-word>` (e.g. `every 20m`, `every 5 minutes`, `every 2 hours`), extract that as the interval and strip it from the prompt. Only match when what follows "every" is a time expression — `check every PR` has no interval.
3. **No interval**: otherwise, the entire input is the prompt and you'll self-pace dynamically (see "Dynamic mode" below).

If the resulting prompt is empty, show usage `/loop [interval] <prompt>` and stop.

Examples:
- `5m /babysit-prs` → interval `5m`, prompt `/babysit-prs` (rule 1)
- `check the deploy every 20m` → interval `20m`, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 2)
- `run tests every 5 minutes` → interval `5m`, prompt `run tests` (rule 2)
- `check the deploy` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 3)
- `check every PR` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check every PR` (rule 3 — "every" not followed by time)
- `5m` → empty prompt → show usage

## Offer cloud first

Before any scheduling step, check whether EITHER is true:
- the parsed interval (rule 1 or 2) is **≥60 minutes**, or
- regardless of which rule matched, the original input uses daily phrasing ("every morning", "daily", "every day", "each night", "every weekday")

If either is true, call AskUserQuestion first:
- `question`: "This loop stops when you close this session. Set it up as a cloud schedule instead so it keeps running?"
- `header`: "Schedule"
- `options`: `[{label: "Cloud schedule (recommended)", description: "Runs in Anthropic's cloud even after you close this session"}, {label: "This session only", description: "Runs in this terminal until you exit"}]`

If they pick **Cloud schedule**: do NOT call CronCreate. Invoke the `schedule` skill directly via the Skill tool with `args` set to their original input verbatim (e.g. `Skill({skill: "schedule", args: "every morning tell me a joke"})`), then follow that skill's instructions to completion. Do NOT tell the user to run /schedule themselves. **Then stop — do not continue to any section below** (no CronCreate, no ScheduleWakeup, no "execute the prompt now").
If they pick **This session only**:
- If the trigger was a parsed ≥60-minute interval (rule 1 or 2): continue below with that interval.
- If the trigger was daily phrasing only (rule 3, no parsed interval): do NOT call CronCreate. Explain that a daily-cadence loop won't fire before this session closes, so there's nothing useful to schedule locally — suggest they either pick Cloud schedule, or re-run `/loop` with an explicit shorter interval (e.g. `/loop 1h <prompt>`) if they want a session loop. Then stop.
If neither trigger condition was met: continue below.

## Fixed-interval mode (rules 1 and 2)

Convert the interval to a cron expression:

| Interval pattern      | Cron expression     | Notes                                    |
|-----------------------|---------------------|------------------------------------------|
| `Nm` where N ≤ 59   | `*/N * * * *`     | every N minutes                          |
| `Nm` where N ≥ 60   | `0 */H * * *`     | round to hours (H = N/60, must divide 24)|
| `Nh` where N ≤ 23   | `0 */N * * *`     | every N hours                            |
| `Nd`                | `0 0 */N * *`     | every N days at midnight local           |
| `Ns`                | treat as `ceil(N/60)m` | cron minimum granularity is 1 minute  |

**If the interval doesn't cleanly divide its unit** (e.g. `7m` → `*/7 * * * *` gives uneven gaps at :56→:00; `90m` → 1.5h which cron can't express), pick the nearest clean interval and tell the user what you rounded to before scheduling.

Then:
1. Call CronCreate with: `cron` (the expression above), `prompt` (the parsed prompt verbatim), `recurring: true`.
2. Briefly confirm: what's scheduled, the cron expression, the human-readable cadence, that recurring tasks auto-expire after 7 days, and that the user can cancel sooner with CronDelete (include the job ID). Only if you did NOT show the cloud-offer AskUserQuestion above (i.e., neither trigger condition applied), end the confirmation with this exact line on its own, italicized: `_Runs until you close this session · For durable cloud-based loops, use /schedule_`. If the user already answered that question, omit this line.
3. **Then immediately execute the parsed prompt now** — don't wait for the first cron fire. If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.

## Dynamic mode (rule 3 — no interval)

The user wants you to self-pace. Decide what makes the next iteration worth running — a passage of time, or an observable event.

1. **Run the parsed prompt now.** If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.
2. **If the next run is gated on an event** (CI finishing, a log line matching, a file changing, a PR comment) and no Monitor is already running for it: arm one now with `persistent: true`. Its events arrive as `<task-notification>` messages and wake this loop immediately — you do not wait for the ScheduleWakeup deadline. Arm once; on later iterations call TaskList first and skip this step if a monitor is already running.
3. **Briefly confirm**: that you're self-pacing, whether a Monitor is the primary wake signal, that you ran the task now, and what fallback delay you're about to pick. Write this as text *before* calling ScheduleWakeup — the turn ends as soon as that tool returns.
4. **Then, as the last action of this turn, call ScheduleWakeup** with:
   - `delaySeconds`: with a Monitor armed this is the **fallback heartbeat** — how long to wait if no event fires (lean 1200–1800s; idle ticks past the 5-minute cache window are pure overhead). Without a Monitor this is the cadence — pick based on what you observed. Read the tool's own description for cache-aware delay guidance.
   - `reason`: one short sentence on why you picked that delay.
   - `prompt`: the full original /loop input verbatim, prefixed with `/loop ` so the next firing re-enters this skill and continues the loop. For example, if the user typed `/loop check the deploy`, pass `/loop check the deploy` as the prompt.
5. **If you were woken by a `<task-notification>`** rather than this prompt: handle the event in the context of the loop task, then call ScheduleWakeup again with the same `prompt` and the same 1200–1800s `delaySeconds` from step 4 — the Monitor remains the wake signal; this only resets the safety net.
6. **To stop the loop**, omit the ScheduleWakeup call and TaskStop any Monitor you armed (use TaskList to find the task ID if it is no longer in context). Before you stop, send a one-line outcome via PushNotification — the user may be away and waiting to hear it's done. Skip this if you're stopping because the user just told you to; they're already here.

> CREATE TABLE public.spaces (
>   id           uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
>   owner_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
>   title        text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Untitled space',
>   pages        jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
>   graph        jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
>   is_template  boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
>   workflow_app jsonb,
>   created_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
>   updated_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
> );
> 
> ALTER TABLE public.spaces ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;   -- default deny
> 
> CREATE POLICY "spaces: select own" ON public.spaces FOR SELECT USING (owner_id = auth.uid());
> CREATE POLICY "spaces: insert own" ON public.spaces FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (owner_id = auth.uid());
> CREATE POLICY "spaces: update own" ON public.spaces FOR UPDATE
>   USING (owner_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (owner_id = auth.uid());
> CREATE POLICY "spaces: delete own" ON public.spaces FOR DELETE USING (owner_id = auth.uid());
> 
> CREATE INDEX spaces_owner_updated_idx ON public.spaces (owner_id, updated_at DESC); -- home list
> 
> CREATE TRIGGER set_spaces_updated_at
>   BEFORE UPDATE ON public.spaces
>   FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();
> 
> COMMENT ON TABLE public.spaces IS
>   'spaces-evolution Fase 1a: infinite-canvas workspace. graph = {nodes, edges, viewport} denormalized (DM-SPACES-002); normalization deferred to realtime collab (FR-SPACES-010).';

Data layer plan\n\n### 2.1 `generations` table — BoK stub verbatim (06-data-model.md:129-150) + canonical hardening\n\nCopy the stub's columns/constraints exactly (BoK wins on semantics):\n\n| Column | Spec (stub) | Ref |\n|---|---|---|\n| `id` | `uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY` | :130 |\n| `owner_id` | `uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` | :131 |\n| `space_id` | `uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.spaces(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` | :132 |\n| `node_id` | `text NOT NULL` | :133 |\n| `node_run_id` | `uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE` — idempotência BR §6 | :134 |\n| `output_type` | `text NOT NULL CHECK (IN 6 PORT_DATA_TYPES: image,text,video,audio,svg,3d)` | :135 |\n| `asset_id` | `uuid REFERENCES public.creative_assets(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` | :136 |\n| `model_key` | `text NOT NULL` | :137 |\n| `mco_charged` | `numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (>= 0)` | :138 |\n| `mco_refunded` | `numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (>= 0 AND <= mco_charged)` — anti-mint, precedente `vision_jobs` | :139 |\n| `usd_cost` / `latency_ms` | nullable | :140-141 |\n| `status` | `text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (IN 6 NODE_STATUSES: idle,pending,running,done,error,blocked)` | :142-143 |\n| `created_at` | `timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()` | :144 |\n\nIndexes per stub: `generations_node_created_idx (node_id, created_at DESC)` (:149), `generations_space_idx (space_id)` (:150). No `embedding` column by design (:153).\n\n**RLS (BoK + canonical agree):** `ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`; exactly one policy `\"generations: select own\" FOR SELECT USING (owner_id = auth.uid())` (:147); **no INSERT/UPDATE policies — writes are service-role only** (:148), mirroring `vision_jobs` (20260615160000:35-42). Migration must pass `/security-review` (06:19,107; FMEA-SPACES-003 08:48).\n\n### 2.2 Canonical-pattern hardening (canonical wins on security) — divergences flagged\n\n| # | Divergence | BoK spec | Canonical pattern | Recommendation |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| D1 | **No `updated_at`** in stub (:144, datamodel gaps) | absent | shared trigger `update_updated_at_column()` (20260615160000:44-47) | **Add `updated_at` + trigger** — SOL-SPACES-002 stuck-run detection (\"running > 30 min\", 08:64) is unsound on `created_at` alone. Canonical wins (operability/security of the refund sweep). |\n| D2 | **No RESTRICTIVE no-delete** | silent | financial family adds `AS RESTRICTIVE FOR DELETE USING (false)` (20260624140000:35-37; 20260620120100:44-45) | **Add it.** Canonical wins; consistent with June-onward ledger convention. |\n| D3 | **Plain FK to spaces** (:132) | `space_id REFERENCES spaces(id)` | composite tenant-scoped FK `(space_id, owner_id) → spaces(id, owner_id)` requires new `UNIQUE (id, owner_id)` anchor on `spaces` (pattern 20260620120100:33-36; spaces lacks anchor — 20260702170000:8-32) | **Add anchor + composite FK.** Canonical wins (a generation can never point at another tenant's space). Note: spaces tenant column is `owner_id`, not `user_id`. |\n| D4 | **No named RPCs** — BoK says only \"finalize_*_render pattern\" + service-role writes (02-brd.md:62,106; 06:148) | direct service-role writes implied | canonical = SECURITY DEFINER RPCs, `SET search_path = ''`, `REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated; GRANT TO service_role` in the same migration (20260615160000:70,105-106; 20260701030000:17-25) | **Create two RPCs** (below). Canonical wins on mechanism; BoK wins on semantics (statuses, column names, action tag). |\n| D5 | **Debit idempotency mechanism unspecified** — 07:52 claims deduct \"idempotente por node_run_id\" but `deduct_mco_coins(p_user_id, p_amount, p_action, p_context)` (20260701010000:97-136) has **no idempotency key**, and the BPMN inserts the generations row only AFTER the provider (07:54-55,120-121), so `node_run_id UNIQUE` cannot stop a concurrent double debit by itself | insert-after-provider | `begin_autopilot_cycle`: insert row + `PERFORM deduct_mco_coins` in the SAME transaction; deduct RAISE rolls the insert back (20260620120200:91-99); idempotency anchor via `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` + `ROW_COUNT` (20260624140000:72-83) | **Ledger-first: canonical wins.** `begin_space_generation` inserts the `status='running'` row keyed on `node_run_id` (conflict → idempotent no-op, zero second debit) then debits in-tx. This also produces the in-flight row FMEA-SPACES-009's sweep needs — resolving the BPMN's own internal contradiction. |\n| D6 | **`numeric` mco columns** vs deduct ledger insert casting `-p_amount::integer` (20260701010000:134) | numeric | integer amounts throughout live RPCs | Keep `numeric` per stub (BoK semantics); all current costs are ints so the cast is latent-only (patterns reader gap). Flag in migration comment. |\n| D7 | **Refund ledger row** — BoK silent; codebase inconsistent (`finalize_autopilot_cycle` writes symmetric `mcoin_transactions` +row 20260620120300:56-61; `finalize_vision_job` does not 20260615160000:96-98) | — | — | **Adopt the autopilot symmetric-row form** (the more complete precedent, per patterns reader) — needed anyway for the KPI-SPACES-004 reconciliation query (`mcoin_transactions × generations`, 08:32). |\n\n### 2.3 New RPCs (service-role-only, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = ''`, fully-qualified refs — 20260603210500:10-14 de-facto rule)\n\n1. **`begin_space_generation(p_owner_id, p_space_id, p_node_id, p_node_run_id, p_model_key, p_output_type, p_amount, p_context)`** — INSERT `generations` row `status='running'`, `mco_charged=p_amount` with `ON CONFLICT (node_run_id) DO NOTHING` + `GET DIAGNOSTICS ROW_COUNT`; if 0 rows → return `{ok:false, reason:'duplicate'}` (idempotent, zero second debit — FMEA-SPACES-002 08:47); else `PERFORM public.deduct_mco_coins(p_owner_id, p_amount::numeric, 'spaces.node.run', p_context)` in the same tx (rollback on RAISE — no orphan row). Advisory lock `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(owner_id::text))` only if the daily-cap aggregate is checked inside the RPC (pattern 20260620120200:48-49); if the cap stays in the edge fn (as live canvas-execute does at index.ts:176-217), the lock is optional — **OPEN** which side owns the cap check.\n2. **`finalize_space_generation(p_node_run_id or p_generation_id, p_status, p_refund, p_asset_id, p_usd_cost, p_latency_ms)`** — guarded claim `UPDATE … WHERE status IN ('pending','running') RETURNING owner_id` (first finalizer wins — 20260615160000:75-93); require terminal status (`done|error`); clamp refund `GREATEST(0, LEAST(p_refund, mco_charged))`; credit via `PERFORM public.add_mco_coins(v_uid, refund)` + symmetric `mcoin_transactions` row (D7); uid always from the claimed row, never from the caller (tenant safety — patterns reader 1c). Bring its own refund bound: nothing downstream catches an oversized credit (`add_mco_coins` has no ceiling/tenant guard — 20260505100000:11-38).\n\nBoth: `REVOKE ALL … FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated; GRANT EXECUTE TO service_role` in the same migration (grant-drift lesson 20260701030000:3-10; drift guard `scripts/qa/audit-privileged-rpc-grants.sh`).\n\n### 2.4 Client-side shape reconciliation\n`GenerationSchema` (src/lib/spaces-schema.ts:130-144) models the cluster card, not the row: it lacks `node_run_id`/`owner_id`, has `mcoRefunded` optional vs `NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`, camelCase vs snake_case. A select-mapper is implied but unspecified (datamodel reader) — **build a trivial row→card mapper in `useGenerationLedger`; do not change the zod schema** (server CHECKs own the invariants).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Execution flow plan\n\n### 3.1 Client orchestration (`useGenerationLedger.runGraph`)\n- **New files:** `src/hooks/useGenerationLedger.ts` (SD-SPACES-003, 05-sdd.md:188-199 — estimate/run/runGraph/clusters; the ONLY client ledger surface, always via edge fn), `src/hooks/useSpaceSession.ts` (SD-SPACES-004, 05:201), `src/lib/spaces-dag.ts` (re-export + types only of `getTopologicalLayers()` from `src/lib/canvas-dag.ts:12`, tested in `src/test/canvas-dag.test.ts` — 05:224).\n- **Sequence** (05-sdd.md:207-227; 07-process-flow.md:34-64): dry-run branch → `projectGraphCost()` (useParamRegistry.ts:81-84) → toast \"Simulação — {n} mcoCoins projetados (sem débito).\" → END, zero server calls (BR-SPACES-005). Else `ensureFresh()` gate → `for layer of getTopologicalLayers(nodes, edges)`: same-layer nodes in parallel (`Promise.allSettled`); per generator node: `updateNodeStatus(pending→running)` → invoke → `done|error` → `invalidate(['generations', nodeId])`. Concurrency ceiling per plan is unquantified (03-prd.md:103) — **OPEN**; rely on server caps for 1b.\n- **Node classification** (no machine-readable flag exists — sop+scaffold gaps): derive per registry: generators (billable) = category image/text with `output` (1b slice: `image-generator`, `image-upscaler`, `prompt-generator`); sources/no-ops = `references, upload, assets, find-inspiration, text` (NA-SPACES-008, 12-node-anatomy:49 — never billed); collector `list` non-billable (NA-SPACES-006); decorative `sticky-note/stickers/group` skipped. **Do NOT trust `data.ports` for typing** — 15 of 26 types get wrong image-generator ports via the `addNode` fallback (useSpacesStore.ts:92; NODE_PORTS covers only 11 types, node-registry.ts:106-124); the executor types edges from the registry + `SpaceEdgeData.dataType`, tolerating untyped hydrated edges (SpaceEdgeGuard allows optional data — spaces-schema.ts:177).\n\n### 3.2 Spaces-type → canvas-execute type mapping (exact strings)\n\nLive implemented server paths: `magic_prompt`, `generate_image`, `scene_compose`, `style_transfer`, `image_to_video` (index.ts:237-256, 313-420, 426-436, 899-902).\n\n| Spaces type | canvas-execute `node_type` | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `image-generator` | `generate_image` | Only server-priced models work: dall-e-3=15, gpt-image-1=18, flux-1.1-pro=12, sdxl=4, flux-schnell=3, gemini-2.5-flash-image=10, higgsfield soul tiers (index.ts:15-44). **None of the 38 Spaces `IMAGE_MODELS` (spaces-schema.ts:43-53) exist in CREDIT_COSTS or provider routing** — they'd price at default 10 then die at \"Unknown provider\" (index.ts:459). |\n| `prompt-generator` | `magic_prompt` | openrouter `google/gemini-2.5-flash` + Gemini BYOK fallback (index.ts:1176-1195). BoK's 'gpt-5-mini' pin (12-node-anatomy:28,43) is the Magnific reference, not a MCORCH mandate — resolve to magic_prompt (Pattern #5 zero-new-tool, 05-sdd.md:316). |\n| `image-upscaler` | **OPEN** | `upscale` is NODE_TYPE_MAP-mapped but has NO dedicated implementation (routes through generic text-to-image); `upscale-2x/4x` cost keys are unreachable (canvas-execute reader gap 7). Either implement a real upscale path in the Spaces branch or **cut from 1b** (honest degrade below). SDD names Upscale in the Fase 1 node set (05:296) — flag at seal if cut. |\n| `text` | — | source, no execute (12:15). |\n| All video/audio/svg/designer/media/utility types (20 remaining) | **unsupported** | Honest degrade: runGraph skips them as non-billable no-ops (sources/decorative) or, for generator types outside the slice (e.g. `video-generator`, `voice-over`, `svg-generator`), surfaces toast \"não suportado na Fase 1\" and leaves status `idle` — never charged, never `error`. Exact UX is **OPEN** (not specified in BoK); the constraint that is specified: non-generator nodes cost 0 (02-brd.md:88-95 rule 3) and no-Run nodes are no-ops (12:49). |\n\n**Model gating:** restrict the 1b executable model list to the server-supported keys (or map `'auto'` → one supported default server-side, which minimally honors Pattern #2 Routing \"Fase 1, junto do executor\" — 05-sdd.md:313). Full `auto` cost/quality routing beyond a default is **OPEN**.\n\n### 3.3 Payload contract (extension of live fn)\nBoK invoke shape `{spaceId, nodeId, node_run_id, model, inputs}` (07:47; 04-frd.md:47) vs live required `{project_id, node_id, prompt, node_type, provider, model, parameters}` (index.ts:76-95,121-123). **Resolution: additive extension** — Spaces client sends the live shape plus `space_id` + `node_run_id`; presence of both switches canvas-execute into \"Spaces ledger mode\" (see §4). Docs claim \"reuso, zero tool novo\" but never acknowledge this required extension (bok:frd+sdd gap) — the SOP must document it.\n\n**Inputs resolution — OPEN:** SpaceNodeDataSchema has no node-level output field (spaces-schema.ts:82-99); outputs live in `generations` rows. How upstream outputs feed `inputs` (which Generation of a batch feeds the child; which of video-generator's 4 out-ports maps where) is UNDEFINED in the BoK (sop+scaffold gap). For the 1b slice (single-output image/text nodes) recommend: latest `done` generation of the upstream node → `input_asset_url`/prompt text, mirroring `useCanvasPipeline.runSingleNode` threading (useCanvasPipeline.ts:69-142). Multi-output mapping deferred with the video nodes.\n\n### 3.4 Status propagation & partial failure\n- Client: `updateNodeStatus` (useSpacesStore.ts:48,129-133) → STATUS_PILL UI already reacts with zero extra wiring (SpaceNodeCard.tsx:9-16,67). `runState idle|running|partial|done` (useSpacesStore.ts:26). Error ⇒ descendants `blocked` via **BFS over outgoing edges** (05:225; 07:58); independent branches continue; final `runState = partial|done`.\n- **Transient-status persistence hazard (must fix in 1b):** `data.status` is part of the persisted graph and the 800ms debounced save deps include `nodes` (SpaceCanvasPage.tsx:26,49-61) — a run writes `running` into `spaces.graph` and reload hydrates stale statuses (code:spaces-live gap). Recommend: strip `status` (or reset transient `pending/running` to `idle`) in `serializeGraph` (useSpaces.ts:101-107) AND/OR call `resetStatuses()` (useSpacesStore.ts:57,161-162) on hydrate. BoK does not specify which — **OPEN**, but doing neither violates G1 semantics.\n- Error mapping (04-frd.md:100-107; 07:150-158): 401 → node `error` (client `refreshSession()` 1×, 0 retries); 422 prompt guards → zero débito; 402 saldo/teto → node `blocked` + canonical strings \"Saldo de mcoCoins insuficiente.\" / \"Teto diário de mcoCoins do dia já atingido.\"; provider fail → 1 server-side retry, fixed 5s backoff, then refund + `error` + `infra_health_logs{spaces_run_failure}`. What `run()` resolves/rejects with on non-200 is undefined (bok:frd+sdd gap) — **OPEN**; recommend structured throw consumed by runGraph for status mapping.\n- First `done` run of a Space → observation node `spaces-first-run-<spaceId>` + `OBSERVES` edge, **fail-open, never blocks** (PROC-SPACES-008, 07:61; FMEA-SPACES-010 08:55).\n\n### 3.5 Idempotency via `node_run_id`\nMinted client-side, uuid per attempt (07:47,111); server dedupes via UNIQUE + `begin_space_generation` ON CONFLICT no-op (§2.3 D5). **Rule to pin in the SOP (unpinned in BoK — datamodel gap):** the client mints `node_run_id` ONCE per node per run attempt and REUSES it on any client-side retry of that same attempt; a new user-initiated re-run mints a new one. A retry that mints a new id defeats idempotency entirely.\n\n### 3.6 Clusters\n`clusters(nodeId) => useInfiniteQuery(['generations', nodeId])` (05:188-199); cursor = **`created_at`** (FRD's `pageParam = updated_at` at 04-frd.md:25 is unsatisfiable — table has only `created_at`, indexed DESC, 06:100,149; DM wins). ≤24 cards/page, staleTime 60s, sentinel + skeletons (04:23-29). Re-run adds (FR-SPACES-012). Card: preview, modelo, `mco_charged`, `refunded_mco` on failure, status, timestamp (FR-SPACES-014). New UI: `GenerationCluster.tsx` (05:32); `QueueIndicator.tsx` (05:33) is async-oriented — with FR-SPACES-013 deferred, minimal topbar runState display suffices for 1b (**scope call, OPEN**).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Billing plan\n\n### 4.1 CRITICAL — canvas-execute already self-bills, in the WRONG order for Spaces\nThe deployed fn charges **after success** (`deduct_mco_coins` at index.ts:375-384 magic_prompt / :585-601 image, actions `canvas_magic_prompt_spend`/`canvas_image_spend`; video via webhook :282-292), with **no pre-debit, no refund path, no request-level idempotency, no `generations` insert, no `spaceId`, no `'spaces.node.run'`** (grep-verified zero matches — bok:frd+sdd reader). The BoK mandates the opposite order: `estimate → reserve → debit → execute → reconcile|refund` (02-brd.md:98-110), debit BEFORE provider (07:52-55).\n\n**Resolution: exclusive Spaces branch inside canvas-execute (extend, not new fn — Pattern #5 \"zero tool novo\", 05-sdd.md:316; 07:108,142).** When `space_id + node_run_id` are present:\n1. JWT verify → prompt guards (422, pre-debit — live at index.ts:50,55) → `CREDIT_COSTS[modelKey]` + balance + daily cap (402) — order per 07:49-51.\n2. `begin_space_generation` (insert `running` row + atomic debit, idempotent — §2.3). Duplicate `node_run_id` → return prior state, **zero second débito** (07:156).\n3. Provider call (1 retry, 5s fixed backoff — 07:155).\n4. Success → `finalize_space_generation(status='done', refund=0, asset_id, usd_cost, latency_ms)` + `register_creative_asset` fail-soft (07:143; live pattern index.ts:546-561). Failure → `finalize_space_generation(status='error', refund=mco_charged)` in the SAME error flow (FMEA-SPACES-001 RPN 144, 08:46) + `infra_health_logs{spaces_run_failure}`.\n5. **The Spaces branch MUST NOT fall through to the legacy deduct-after-success block** — that is the double-charge hazard. The two billing paths are mutually exclusive by branch; legacy Canvas Studio behavior is untouched.\n\n### 4.2 Amounts and exemptions\n- Server authority = `CREDIT_COSTS` in canvas-execute (index.ts:15-44); client `CLIENT_COST_MIRROR` display-only (useParamRegistry.ts:26-39). LLM nodes dynamic via `calc_agent_cost_mcoin` per BR-SPACES-002 — live magic_prompt uses level-based 1/2/5 (index.ts:149-162) — divergence, **OPEN** (recommend: keep live magic_prompt pricing for 1b; it is the existing tool).\n- **BYOK** (BR-SPACES-006): `byok=true` skips `deduct_mco_coins`; residual \"taxa de orquestração/análise\" amount undefined — **OPEN**, recommend 0 for 1b (client mirror already returns 0, useParamRegistry.ts:76). Record the generation with `mco_charged=0`; no byok column exists in the table (datamodel gap) — visible only via zero charge.\n- Non-generator nodes cost 0 (02:88-95 rule 3). Batch: estimate = base × batch (useParamRegistry.ts:75-79, zod 1..8); server-side batch semantics vs `node_run_id` are undefined (sop+scaffold gap) — **OPEN**; note the live Soul batch pays 4× and delivers 1 image (index.ts:904-908,972) — do not expose batch>1 billing in 1b without fixing.\n\n### 4.3 Daily cap composition\nLive cap sums `mcoin_transactions` where `action LIKE 'canvas_%spend'`, role-based 100/1000/10000 (index.ts:176-217; comment :585 \"action tagging is REQUIRED\"). `'spaces.node.run'` does NOT match that LIKE — **the cap query must be extended to include the new action or Spaces spend silently bypasses the cap.** BRD ties caps to plans (BR-SPACES-003/010) vs live role-based — contradiction; resolve: keep the live role-based mechanism for 1b (BR-SPACES-010 forbids a parallel billing system), record the plan-based model as **OPEN** debt.\n\n### 4.4 Refund + reconciliation\nRefund = positive credit via `add_mco_coins` inside `finalize_space_generation`, clamped by the `mco_refunded <= mco_charged` CHECK, idempotent by claimed-UPDATE (first finalizer wins), + symmetric `mcoin_transactions` row (§2 D7). Stuck rows (`running` > 30 min) → SOL-SPACES-002: mark `error` + idempotent refund, NUNCA re-cobrar (08:64; FMEA-SPACES-009) — executed by `scripts/self-heal-spaces.sh` stub \"nasce com a Fase 1\" (08:86-93), run on-demand (no new cron — 07:182). KPI-SPACES-004 (violations = 0, P0 freeze — 08:32,111) reconciles `mcoin_transactions action='spaces.node.run'` × `generations`; the join key is unspecified in the BoK (datamodel gap) — **resolve: put `node_run_id` in `p_context`** of every deduct/refund row.\n\n**Activation gate:** zero-cost smoke of 401/402/422/refund branches BEFORE any paid run (08:153; FMEA-SPACES-001 action).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Contradictions & gaps (with recommended resolutions)\n\n| # | Contradiction / gap | Resolution |\n|---|---|---|\n| C1 | Debit order: BoK debit-before-provider (07:52) vs live canvas-execute deduct-after-success (index.ts:585-601) | Exclusive Spaces branch adopts BoK order with begin/finalize RPCs; legacy paths untouched; branches mutually exclusive (no double charge). BoK wins on semantics, canonical RPC pattern wins on mechanics. |\n| C2 | BPMN inserts `generations` AFTER provider (07:54-55) vs sweep needing a `running` row (08:64) and `node_run_id UNIQUE` needing a row to dedupe against | Ledger-first `begin_space_generation` (insert running row + debit same tx). Canonical wins — resolves the BoK's internal contradiction. |\n| C3 | 07:52 claims `deduct_mco_coins` \"idempotente por node_run_id\"; the RPC has no idempotency key (20260701010000:97-136) | Idempotency lives in `generations.node_run_id UNIQUE` via the begin RPC, not in deduct. |\n| C4 | Payload: `{spaceId, nodeId, model, inputs}` (04:47) vs live `{project_id, node_id, prompt, …}` (index.ts:121-123) | Additive extension (`space_id`, `node_run_id`); document in the new SOP. |\n| C5 | Table name `generation` (04-frd.md:88) vs `generations` (06:84,129) | `generations` — DM stub is authoritative. |\n| C6 | Cluster cursor `updated_at` (04:25) vs table has only `created_at` (06:100,149) | `created_at` DESC. |\n| C7 | Daily cap plan-based (BRD) vs role-based live (index.ts:179-212); new action not matched by `canvas_%spend` LIKE | Keep role-based for 1b; extend the cap query to include `'spaces.node.run'`. Plan-based = OPEN debt. |\n| C8 | FR-SPACES-010 collab Fase 2 (04:78) vs Fase 3 (05:298, 06:82) | Beyond 1b either way; note for BoK errata. |\n| C9 | `GenerationSchema` zod vs table drift (no node_run_id/owner_id; mcoRefunded optional; camelCase) — spaces-schema.ts:130-144 vs 06:129-150 | Zod = cluster card; add row→card mapper in `useGenerationLedger`; server CHECKs own invariants. |\n| C10 | prompt-generator pinned 'gpt-5-mini' (12:28,43) vs MCORCH `magic_prompt` gemini path | Use existing `magic_prompt` (zero new tool). |\n| C11 | 38 Spaces IMAGE_MODELS have zero server pricing/routing (spaces-schema.ts:43-53 vs index.ts:15-44,459); CLIENT_COST_MIRROR keys don't exist server-side (useParamRegistry.ts:27-39) | 1b executes only server-supported keys (+`'auto'`→ supported default); ship CI mirror-parity test (SOL-SPACES-001, FMEA-SPACES-004 RPN 144) and prune/align the mirror. |\n| C12 | `image-upscaler` in Fase 1 node set (05:296) but server has no real upscale implementation; upscale-2x/4x cost keys unreachable | OPEN scope call: implement a minimal upscale path in the Spaces branch OR cut it and declare at seal. |\n| C13 | Port truth conflict: `addNode` imageGeneratorPorts fallback mistypes 15 of 26 types (useSpacesStore.ts:92; NODE_PORTS covers 11 — node-registry.ts:106-124); NODE_TYPES vs NODE_PORTS disagree for `list` (node-registry.ts:53 vs :116-119) | Executor derives typing from registry + edge `dataType`, never trusts stored ports; NODE_PORTS wins for handles. OPEN. |\n| C20 | Node-output → child-input resolution undefined (no output field; multi-output nodes unmapped) | 1b: single-output convention = latest `done` generation of upstream node; multi-output deferred with video nodes. Slice cut — minimal honest Fase 1b (one loop iteration)\n\n**Build now (ordered):**\n1. **SOP** `docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md` — Operator/Sequence/Verification/Recovery/Success, documenting: the canvas-execute additive extension (C4), debit order (C1), node_run_id mint rule (C23), cap-query extension (C7), status-strip rule (C14). Lei 2 gate — before any code.\n2. **Migration** — `generations` per stub 06:129-150 verbatim + hardening: `updated_at`+trigger (D1), RESTRICTIVE no-delete (D2), `UNIQUE (id, owner_id)` anchor on `spaces` + composite tenant FK (D3), `begin_space_generation` + `finalize_space_generation` (D4/D5, `search_path=''`, REVOKE/GRANT trailer). **`/security-review` mandatory.**\n3. **canvas-execute Spaces branch** — `space_id`+`node_run_id` trigger mode; gate order JWT→422 guards→402 balance/cap→begin(debit)→provider (1 retry/5s)→finalize (done | error+full refund); action `'spaces.node.run'` with `node_run_id` in context; cap query extended; `register_creative_asset` fail-soft; telemetry `spaces_run_failure`/`spaces_refund_issued` (08:73-82). Node types: `generate_image` (server-supported models + `auto`→default) and `magic_prompt` only; `image-upscaler` in only if a real path is cheap, else declared cut (C12).\n4. **Client** — `src/hooks/useGenerationLedger.ts` (estimate/run/runGraph/clusters), `src/hooks/useSpaceSession.ts`, `src/lib/spaces-dag.ts` re-export; Run ▶ + dry-run button beside \"Adicionar nó\" (SpaceCanvasPage.tsx:129-131); layer loop + BFS blocked + runState; canonical 402 strings; transient-status fix (C14); `GenerationCluster.tsx` lazy ≤24/`created_at` cursor; card with `mco_charged`/`refunded_mco` (FR-SPACES-014).\n5. **Gates before activation** — zero-cost smoke 401/402/422/refund + duplicate-`node_run_id` (08:153); CI mirror-parity test (SOL-SPACES-001); `scripts/self-heal-spaces.sh` stub (08:86-93, on-demand, no cron); first-run mesh observation fail-open (PROC-SPACES-008); Pattern Conformance re-declaration flipping #1/#2(minimal auto-default)/#3/#12 + #11/#19 at seal (05:334).\n\n**Defer explicitly (declare at seal):** video/audio/svg/designer execution + FR-SPACES-013 async/QueueIndicator; batch>1 billing (C18 Soul hazard); BYOK residual fee; plan-based caps; `auto` full routing; `erase_space_artifacts` (OTD-SPACES-001, named SLA); cancellation; multi-output port mapping; references picker (already \"Fase 2\", ParamHUD.tsx:50); zundo/viewport 1a debts (C22).",

> 141	 * Trace: NA-SPACES-002, SD-SPACES-006.
> 142	 */
> 143	export function buildPorts(typeId: string): Port[] {
> 144	  const def = getNodeType(typeId);
> 145	  const spec = NODE_PORTS[resolveNodeType(typeId)];
> 146	  if (!spec) return [];
> 147	  const ports: Port[] = [];
> 148	  for (const id of spec.in) {

> 151	  for (const id of spec.out) {

> 131	      </Button>

> 133	    </div>

> 135	}

> ```
> 1	import type { CanvasNode } from "@/types/canvas";
> 2	import type { Edge } from "@xyflow/react";
> 3	
> 4	/**
> 5	 * Implements Kahn's algorithm to sort Canvas nodes into topological layers.
> 6	 * Nodes within the same layer can be executed in parallel because they have no
> 7	 * dependencies on each other or their dependencies have already been satisfied
> 8	 * in previous layers.
> 9	 *
> 10	 * Throws an error if a cycle is detected.
> 11	 */
> 12	export function getTopologicalLayers(
> 13	  nodes: CanvasNode[],
> 14	  edges: Edge[]
> 15	): CanvasNode[][] {
> 16	  const nodeMap = new Map<string, CanvasNode>();
> 17	  for (const node of nodes) {
> 18	    nodeMap.set(node.id, node);
> 19	  }
> 20	
> 21	  const adj = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
> 22	  const inDegree = new Map<string, number>();
> 23	
> 24	  for (const node of nodes) {
> 25	    adj.set(node.id, new Set<string>());
> 26	    inDegree.set(node.id, 0);
> 27	  }
> 28	
> 29	  for (const edge of edges) {
> 30	    if (nodeMap.has(edge.source) && nodeMap.has(edge.target)) {
> 31	      const targets = adj.get(edge.source)!;
> 32	      if (!targets.has(edge.target)) {
> 33	        targets.add(edge.target);
> 34	        inDegree.set(edge.target, inDegree.get(edge.target)! + 1);
> 35	      }
> 36	    }
> 37	  }
> 38	
> 39	  const layers: CanvasNode[][] = [];
> 40	  let currentQueue = nodes.filter((n) => inDegree.get(n.id) === 0);
> 41	  let processedCount = 0;
> 42	
> 43	  while (currentQueue.length > 0) {
> 44	    layers.push(currentQueue);
> 45	    processedCount += currentQueue.length;
> 46	
> 47	    const nextQueue: CanvasNode[] = [];
> 48	    for (const node of currentQueue) {
> 49	      const neighbors = adj.get(node.id)!;
> 50	      for (const neighborId of neighbors) {
> 51	        const currentInDegree = inDegree.get(neighborId)! - 1;
> 52	        inDegree.set(neighborId, currentInDegree);
> 53	        if (currentInDegree === 0) {
> 54	          nextQueue.push(nodeMap.get(neighborId)!);
> 55	        }
> 56	      }
> 57	    }
> 58	    currentQueue = nextQueue;
> 59	  }
> 60	
> 61	  if (processedCount < nodes.length) {
> 62	    throw new Error("Cycle detected in Canvas Graph");
> 63	  }
> 64	
> 65	  return layers;
> 66	}
> 67	
> ```

> 1	# SOP — Spaces Canvas: criação, edição e persistência (Fase 1a)
> 2	
> 3	> **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado).** SOP do fluxo humano que a Fase 1a do Spaces automatiza —
> 4	> detalha os processos `PROC-SPACES-001/002/006` da BoK (`docs/bok/spaces-evolution/07-process-flow.md`)
> 5	> no nível de operação. Execução/ledger (PROC-SPACES-003) é a Fase 1b — fora deste SOP.

> 7	
> 8	## Operator
> 9	
> 10	**Sovereign (Gabriel)** como Usuário Zero — abre `/dashboard/spaces`, cria/edita canvases.
> 11	
> 12	## Sequence
> 13	
> 14	1. `/dashboard/spaces` → lista os Spaces do tenant (RLS-own) com nome, nº de nós e updated_at;
> 15	   empty-state pt-BR canônico (*"Nenhum Space ainda…"*, FR-SPACES-001).
> 16	2. **Criar** → dialog de nome → INSERT em `spaces` (graph vazio) → navega para `/dashboard/spaces/:id`.
> 17	3. **Canvas** (`SpaceCanvasPage`): hidrata o grafo de `spaces.graph` (jsonb) no `useSpacesStore`
> 18	   — nós/edges passam pelos guards zod ANTES de entrar no store (`FMEA-SPACES-007`); linha de
> 19	   outro tenant → RLS devolve vazio → tela "Space não encontrado" (404-like, `FR-SPACES-007`).
> 20	4. **Inserir nó**: Spotlight (`N` ou `/`) → busca nos 26 tipos canônicos → `⏎` insere no centro
> 21	   da viewport, auto-selecionado, título auto-numerado (`PROC-SPACES-001`).
> 22	5. **Conectar**: arrastar porta→porta → `canConnect` (única fonte de verdade) valida os 6 tipos;
> 23	   incompatível → toast pt-BR *"Tipos incompatíveis: {a} → {b}"* e a edge NÃO entra (`PROC-SPACES-002`).
> 24	6. **Editar parâmetros**: HUD direito 72/28 (nó selecionado → schema de `useParamRegistry`).
> 25	7. **Persistir**: toda mutação → debounce 800 ms → UPDATE de `spaces.graph`
> 26	   (`PROC-SPACES-006`) com **guard empty-over-nonempty** (nunca sobrescrever grafo cheio com
> 27	   vazio — anticorpo do incidente Canvas 2026-06-25 / `FMEA-SPACES-006`).
> 28	
> 29	## Verification gates
> 30	
> 31	| Gate | Critério material |
> 32	|---|---|
> 33	| G1 — persistência real | Criar space + 2 nós + 1 edge → reload → grafo volta; provado pela ROW no DB (`spaces.graph` jsonb com os ids), não pelo DOM. |
> 34	| G2 — porta tipada | Conexão `image→text` rejeitada com toast; `text→text` aceita (smoke de store + browser). |
> 35	| G3 — anti-perda | Save com store vazio ANTES da hidratação não sobrescreve grafo cheio (guard provado por teste/verificação). |
> 36	| G4 — tenant | `spaces` RLS-own 4 policies; abrir id de outro tenant → "não encontrado" (sem vazamento). |
> 37	| G5 — zero débito | Fase 1a não toca ledger: nenhuma chamada a `deduct_mco_coins`/`canvas-execute`. |
> 38	
> 39	## Recovery path

> 41	- Falha no UPDATE → toast pt-BR + estado local preservado (zundo buffer); retry no próximo debounce.
> 42	- Grafo corrompido no DB → guards zod dropam apenas os itens inválidos (console.warn) e o canvas abre
> 43	  com o resto — nunca white-screen (`FMEA-SPACES-005`: lookups sempre com fallback).

> 194	    const dayAgoIso = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();

> 4	-- SOP: docs/processes/vision-mcp-deepsearch-async.md (Lei 2 — written before this code).
> 5	--
> 6	-- deepsearch.run debits the full class (3 mco) on entry, INSERTs a 'queued' row here, kicks an in-process
> 7	-- worker, and returns job_id immediately (NEVER blocking). deepsearch.poll (cost 0, tenant-bound) reads state.
> 8	-- The worker computes the partial-delivery refund (§4.5: floor in the tenant's favor) and writes refunded_mco
> 9	-- + refunded_at (the once-per-job idempotency guard).
> 10	--
> 11	-- Tenancy: SELECT-own only (poll/dashboard). The container is the ONLY writer — via the service key (bypasses
> 12	-- RLS). NO INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policy for authenticated → default-deny: a tenant can never forge nor mutate a
> 13	-- job (so a tenant cannot fabricate a 'done' job to read someone else's result, nor zero its own charge).
> 14	-- Mirrors the marketing-hub lead_core tenancy pattern (20260603000000). Passes /security-review (FMEA-011).
> 15	
> 16	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.vision_jobs (
> 17	  id              uuid        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),       -- = job_id do contrato FR-VM-007
> 18	  user_id         uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 19	  project_id      uuid,                                                    -- optional tenant project (no projects table)
> 20	  tool            text        NOT NULL,                                    -- 'deepsearch.run'
> 21	  state           text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued'
> 22	                    CHECK (state IN ('queued','running','done','partial','failed')),
> 23	  planned_units   int         NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (planned_units >= 1),   -- cap por plano app-side (DD-VM-003)
> 24	  delivered_units int         NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (delivered_units >= 0),
> 25	  charged_mco     int         NOT NULL CHECK (charged_mco >= 0),
> 26	  refunded_mco    int         CHECK (refunded_mco IS NULL OR (refunded_mco >= 0 AND refunded_mco <= charged_mco)),
> 27	  refunded_at     timestamptz,                                             -- idempotency guard (refund 1×/job — §4.5.6)
> 28	  failed_units    jsonb,
> 29	  result          jsonb,
> 30	  expires_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT (now() + interval '7 days'),-- poll retention ≥24h (NFR-VM-003)
> 31	  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
> 32	  updated_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
> 33	);
> 34	
> 35	ALTER TABLE public.vision_jobs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 36	
> 37	-- SELECT own only (§5.2). No write policies → authenticated default-deny; the container writes via service key.
> 38	CREATE POLICY "vision_jobs_select_own" ON public.vision_jobs
> 39	  FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
> 40	
> 41	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS vision_jobs_user_state_idx ON public.vision_jobs (user_id, state);
> 42	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS vision_jobs_expires_idx    ON public.vision_jobs (expires_at);
> 43	
> 44	-- Maintain updated_at on every write (shared trigger fn used across the schema, e.g. lead_core).
> 45	CREATE TRIGGER tr_update_vision_jobs_updated_at
> 46	  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.vision_jobs
> 47	  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();
> 48	
> 49	COMMENT ON TABLE public.vision_jobs IS
> 50	  'Vision MCP async job state machine (deepsearch.run/poll — FR-VM-007). queued→running→done|partial|failed; '
> 51	  'partial-delivery refund §4.5 (refunded_mco/refunded_at). SELECT-own RLS; container writes via service key.';
> 52	
> 53	-- ── Atomic finalize + refund authority (closes the /security-review HIGH: charge-without-value + refund/guard
> 54	--    atomicity). The ONLY way a job goes terminal + refunds. Guard `state IN (queued,running)` ⇒ exactly the
> 55	--    FIRST finalizer wins (worker OR the poll lazy-reconcile OR a future sweep) — idempotent: a second call on
> 56	--    an already-terminal job is a no-op (no double refund). The vision_jobs guard write AND the balance credit
> 57	--    (add_mco_coins) commit in ONE transaction ⇒ never money-without-guard nor guard-without-money (§4.5.6).
> 58	--    service_role-only (the container calls it via SB_SECRET_KEY; tenants never reach it). FMEA-011 reviewed.
> 59	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_vision_job(
> 60	  p_job_id    uuid,
> 61	  p_state     text,
> 62	  p_delivered int,
> 63	  p_amount    int,    -- refund to credit (0 = no refund, e.g. 'done')
> 64	  p_failed    jsonb,
> 65	  p_result    jsonb
> 66	)
> 67	RETURNS jsonb
> 68	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 69	SECURITY DEFINER
> 70	SET search_path = ''
> 71	AS $$
> 72	DECLARE
> 73	  v_uid uuid;
> 74	BEGIN
> 75	  IF p_state NOT IN ('done','partial','failed') THEN
> 76	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'finalize_vision_job: terminal state required, got %', p_state USING errcode = '22023';
> 77	  END IF;
> 78	
> 79	  -- Claim + finalize a NON-TERMINAL job exactly once.
> 80	  UPDATE public.vision_jobs
> 81	     SET state           = p_state,
> 82	         delivered_units = p_delivered,
> 83	         result          = p_result,
> 84	         failed_units    = p_failed,
> 85	         refunded_mco    = CASE WHEN p_amount > 0 THEN p_amount ELSE refunded_mco END,
> 86	         refunded_at     = CASE WHEN p_amount > 0 THEN now()  ELSE refunded_at END
> 87	   WHERE id = p_job_id
> 88	     AND state IN ('queued','running')
> 89	   RETURNING user_id INTO v_uid;
> 90	
> 91	  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
> 92	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', false);   -- already terminal → no-op (no double refund)
> 93	  END IF;
> 94	
> 95	  -- Guard write (above) + balance credit commit in the SAME transaction.
> 96	  IF p_amount > 0 THEN
> 97	    PERFORM public.add_mco_coins(v_uid, p_amount);
> 98	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', true, 'refunded', p_amount);
> 99	  END IF;
> 100	
> 101	  RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', true, 'refunded', 0);
> 102	END;
> 103	$$;
> 104	
> 105	REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.finalize_vision_job(uuid, text, int, int, jsonb, jsonb) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
> 106	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.finalize_vision_job(uuid, text, int, int, jsonb, jsonb) TO service_role;
> 107	
> ```

```json
  "content": "# SOP \u2014 Spaces: execu\u00e7\u00e3o do grafo com ledger (Fase 1b)\n\n> **Lei 2 (Processo Antecipado).** SOP do fluxo humano que a Fase 1b do Spaces automatiza \u2014\n> detalha `PROC-SPACES-003/007/008` da BoK (`docs/bok/spaces-evolution/07-process-flow.md`) no n\u00edvel\n> de opera\u00e7\u00e3o, e SELA as resolu\u00e7\u00f5es das contradi\u00e7\u00f5es BoK\u2194c\u00f3digo-vivo encontradas no mapeamento\n> (workflow `wf_b4abf175-410`, 2026-07-02). Criada ANTES do c\u00f3digo (loop aut\u00f4nomo).\n> Sibling: `spaces-canvas-persistence.md` (Fase 1a \u2014 gates G1-G5 permanecem v\u00e1lidos e inviol\u00e1veis).\n\n## Operator\n\n**Sovereign (Gabriel)** como Usu\u00e1rio Zero \u2014 monta um grafo em `/dashboard/spaces/:id`, estima o\ncusto (dry-run), roda (\u25b6) e colhe as gera\u00e7\u00f5es no cluster do n\u00f3, com d\u00e9bito/estorno corretos de mcoCoins.\n\n## Decis\u00f5es seladas (resolu\u00e7\u00f5es de contradi\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 fonte: s\u00edntese `wf_b4abf175-410`)\n\n| # | Decis\u00e3o | Racional |\n|---|---------|----------|\n| S1 | **Ordem de d\u00e9bito = ledger-first**: `begin_space_generation` INSERE a row `generations` `status='running'` **e** debita (`deduct_mco_coins`, action `spaces.node.run`) **na MESMA transa\u00e7\u00e3o**; falha do deduct \u2192 rollback da row (nunca \u00f3rf\u00e3). | Resolve a contradi\u00e7\u00e3o interna da BoK (BPMN insere ap\u00f3s provider vs sweep precisa de row `running`); espelha `begin_autopilot_cycle`. |\n| S2 | **Idempot\u00eancia** vive em `generations.node_run_id UNIQUE` + `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` no begin (0 rows \u2192 retorna estado pr\u00e9vio, **zero segundo d\u00e9bito**) \u2014 N\u00c3O no `deduct_mco_coins` (que n\u00e3o tem chave de idempot\u00eancia). | C3/C5 da s\u00edntese. |\n| S3 | **`node_run_id` \u00e9 cunhado no cliente 1\u00d7 por n\u00f3 por tentativa de run** (`crypto.randomUUID()`); retry client-side da MESMA tentativa REUSA o id; re-run iniciado pelo usu\u00e1rio cunha id novo. | Retry que cunha id novo derrota a idempot\u00eancia (C23). |\n| S4 | **Reuso do `canvas-execute` (zero tool novo, Pattern #5)** via **extens\u00e3o aditiva**: presen\u00e7a de `space_id` + `node_run_id` no payload ativa o \"Spaces ledger mode\" (branch exclusivo). O caminho legado (deduct-after-success do Canvas Studio) fica INTOCADO; os dois branches s\u00e3o mutuamente exclusivos \u2014 sem double-charge. | C1/C4. |\n| S5 | **Teto di\u00e1rio**: a query do cap passa de `LIKE 'canvas_%spend'` para lista expl\u00edcita `IN ('canvas_magic_prompt_spend','canvas_image_spend','canvas_video_spend','spaces.node.run')` \u2014 sen\u00e3o o gasto Spaces bypassa o teto silenciosamente. Modelo por-role (100/1000/10000) mantido; teto por-plano = d\u00e9bito OPEN (BR-SPACES-003). | C7. |\n| S6 | **Refund = cr\u00e9dito positivo** via `finalize_space_generation` (claim `status IN ('pending','running')` \u2014 primeiro finalizador vence, idempotente), clamp `0 \u2264 refund \u2264 mco_charged`, `add_mco_coins` + **row sim\u00e9trica** em `mcoin_transactions` (action `spaces.node.refund`) com `node_run_id` no context (chave da reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o KPI-SPACES-004). uid SEMPRE da row reclamada, nunca do caller. | D5/D7/C15. |\n| S7 | **Escopo execut\u00e1vel 1b**: `image-generator` \u2192 `generate_image` e `prompt-generator` \u2192 `magic_prompt`. **`image-upscaler` CORTADO da 1b** (o server n\u00e3o tem caminho real de upscale \u2014 `upscale-2x/4x` s\u00e3o cost-keys inalcan\u00e7\u00e1veis); declarado no seal. Demais tipos: fontes/decorativos = no-op (nunca cobrados); geradores fora do slice (v\u00eddeo/\u00e1udio/svg/designer) \u2192 toast pt-BR \"n\u00e3o suportado na Fase 1\" + status `idle` (nunca `error`, nunca cobrado). | C12 + NA-SPACES-008. |\n| S8 | **Mapa de modelos**: os 38 `IMAGE_MODELS` do Spaces s\u00e3o chaves Magnific SEM roteamento server. 1b resolve por `SPACES_MODEL_MAP` client-side: `auto` \u2192 `openrouter`/`google/gemini-2.5-flash-image` (10 mco); `flux.1.1` \u2192 `openrouter`/`black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro` (12 mco). Chave fora do mapa \u2192 resolve como `auto` (Pattern #2 Routing m\u00ednimo) e o `estimateCost` do HUD usa o pre\u00e7o do `auto` como fallback (estimativa = cobran\u00e7a). A row `generations.model_key` grava a chave **resolvida** (`provider/model` \u2014 verdade do server). | C11. |\n| S9 | **Status transit\u00f3rio n\u00e3o persiste**: o serializer da Fase 1a passa a resetar `pending/running` \u2192 `idle` ao persistir `spaces.graph` (reload nunca hidrata run fantasma). `done/error/blocked` podem persistir (sinal hist\u00f3rico). | C14 \u2014 sem isso viola G1 da SOP 1a. |\n| S10 | **Batch N = N execu\u00e7\u00f5es independentes** do mesmo n\u00f3 (cada uma com `node_run_id` e row pr\u00f3prios, sequenciais). Estimativa `base \u00d7 batch` = cobran\u00e7a real. NUNCA reusar o batch nativo do provider (hazard Soul paga-4-entrega-1, C18). | C18. |\n| S11 | **Threading de inputs (conven\u00e7\u00e3o single-output 1b)**: edge chegando no handle `prompt` + upstream com gera\u00e7\u00e3o `done` de texto \u2192 o texto vira o prompt do filho (fallback: prompt pr\u00f3prio); edge no handle `reference` + upstream `done` de imagem \u2192 `reference_image_urls=[url]`. A sa\u00edda de texto mora em `generations.result` (jsonb \u2014 diverg\u00eancia aditiva D8 do stub, precedente `vision_jobs.result`); imagem mora em `creative_assets`/URL. Multi-output = diferido com os n\u00f3s de v\u00eddeo. | C20. |\n| S12 | **Billing segue o precedente vivo**: cobra `CREDIT_COSTS` mesmo com chave BYOK do tenant (todo canvas-execute j\u00e1 \u00e9 per-user BYOK e cobra \u2014 mcoCoins \u00e9 cobran\u00e7a de valor de plataforma). \"BYOK isenta d\u00e9bito\" (BR-SPACES-006) contradiz o precedente do ecossistema inteiro \u2192 registrado como errata/OPEN de BoK no seal; `data.byok` fica dormant. | C17. |\n| S13 | Colunas mco em `numeric` (sem\u00e2ntica BoK); todos os custos atuais s\u00e3o inteiros \u2014 o cast `::integer` do ledger \u00e9 latente-only (comentado na migration). | D6. |\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. **Montar o grafo** (Fase 1a): n\u00f3s `prompt-generator` e/ou `image-generator` conectados por portas\n   tipadas; par\u00e2metros no HUD (modelo, prompt, propor\u00e7\u00e3o, batch).\n2. **Dry-run (estimar)**: bot\u00e3o \"Estimar\" \u2192 `projectGraphCost()` client-side \u2192 toast\n   *\"Simula\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 {n} mcoCoins projetados (sem d\u00e9bito).\"* \u2192 **zero chamadas de servidor** (BR-SPACES-005).\n3. **Run \u25b6**: `useSpaceSession.ensureFresh()` (sess\u00e3o expirada \u2192 1 refresh; falha \u2192 toast + aborta);\n   `getTopologicalLayers(nodes, edges)` (Kahn \u2014 ciclo \u2192 erro pt-BR); por camada, n\u00f3s execut\u00e1veis em\n   paralelo (`Promise.allSettled`); por n\u00f3 gerador: status `pending`\u2192`running` \u2192 invoke\n   `canvas-execute` `{...payload legado, space_id, node_run_id}` \u2192 `done|error` \u2192\n   `invalidateQueries(['generations', nodeId])`.\n4. **Servidor (branch Spaces)**: JWT \u2192 guards 422 (prompt) \u2192 402 saldo/teto (strings can\u00f4nicas) \u2192\n   `begin_space_generation` (S1/S2) \u2192 provider (1 retry, backoff fixo 5s) \u2192 sucesso:\n   `finalize_space_generation('done', refund=0, asset_id, result, usd, latency)` +\n   `register_creative_asset` fail-soft; falha: `finalize_space_generation('error', refund=mco_charged)`\n   + `infra_health_logs{event:'spaces_run_failure'}`.\n5. **Falha parcial**: n\u00f3 `error` \u2192 descendentes `blocked` (BFS pelas edges de sa\u00edda); ramos\n   independentes continuam; `runState` final = `partial|done`.\n6. **Cluster**: `useInfiniteQuery(['generations', nodeId])` \u2014 \u226424 cards/p\u00e1gina, cursor `created_at`\n   DESC, `staleTime` 60s; card mostra preview, modelo, `mco_charged`, `refunded_mco` (se estorno),\n   status, timestamp. Re-run ADICIONA, nunca sobrescreve (FR-SPACES-012).\n7. **Primeira gera\u00e7\u00e3o `done` de um Space** \u2192 n\u00f3 `observation` `spaces-first-run-<spaceId>` na\n   Knowledge Mesh, **fail-open** (nunca bloqueia o run \u2014 PROC-SPACES-008).\n\n## Verification gates\n\n| Gate | Crit\u00e9rio material |\n|---|---|\n| G1 \u2014 ledger-first at\u00f4mico | `begin_space_generation` com saldo insuficiente \u2192 EXCEPTION do deduct \u2192 **zero row** em `generations` (rollback provado); com saldo \u2192 row `running` + d\u00e9bito na mesma tx (row de `mcoin_transactions` action `spaces.node.run` com `node_run_id` no context). |\n| G2 \u2014 idempot\u00eancia | 2\u00aa chamada com o MESMO `node_run_id` \u2192 `duplicate:true`, **zero segundo d\u00e9bito** (saldo intacto entre as duas chamadas \u2014 provado por SELECT). |\n| G3 \u2014 refund idempotente | `finalize('error', refund)` \u2192 saldo restaurado + row sim\u00e9trica `spaces.node.refund`; 2\u00aa chamada de finalize na mesma row \u2192 no-op (`finalized:false`), sem double-refund. CHECK `mco_refunded \u2264 mco_charged` no schema (anti-mint). |\n| G4 \u2014 guards zero-custo | 401 sem JWT \u00b7 402 saldo \u00b7 402 teto \u00b7 422 prompt-guard \u2192 **nenhuma** row nova em `generations` e saldo intacto. |\n| G5 \u2014 tenant | `generations` RLS SELECT-own only (sem policy de INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE p/ authenticated); FK composta `(space_id, owner_id)` \u2192 gera\u00e7\u00e3o nunca aponta pro space de outro tenant; RESTRICTIVE no-delete. `/security-review` obrigat\u00f3rio na migration. |\n| G6 \u2014 smoke antes de run pago | O smoke zero-cost (G1-G5) passa ANTES de qualquer run pago real (FMEA-SPACES-001, 08:153). |\n| G7 \u2014 UI honesta | Estimativa do HUD = cobran\u00e7a real p/ o conjunto execut\u00e1vel (mirror-parity dos keys do S8); n\u00f3 n\u00e3o-suportado nunca cobra nem vira `error`. |\n\n## Recovery path\n\n- **Provider falhou (ap\u00f3s retry)**: finalize `error` + refund total autom\u00e1tico (S6) \u2014 nunca \"tente de novo\" sem estorno.\n- **Run travado** (`running` > 30 min \u2014 crash do edge fn entre begin e finalize):\n  `scripts/self-heal-spaces.sh` (on-demand, SEM cron novo \u2014 07:182) marca `error` + refund\n  idempotente via `finalize_space_generation`. NUNCA re-cobrar.\n- **Duplicidade suspeita**: reconciliar `mcoin_transactions` (actions `spaces.node.run`/`spaces.node.refund`,\n  join por `context->>'node_run_id'`) \u00d7 `generations` \u2014 invariante KPI-SPACES-004: \u03a3 d\u00e9bitos = \u03a3 `mco_charged`\n  de rows n\u00e3o-estornadas; viola\u00e7\u00e3o = P0 freeze.\n- **Sess\u00e3o expirada no meio do run**: n\u00f3 atual \u2192 `error` (com refund server-side se j\u00e1 debitado);\n  cliente tenta `refreshSession()` 1\u00d7; camadas seguintes abortam com toast.\n\n## Success signal\n\nSovereign roda \u25b6 num grafo `prompt-generator \u2192 image-generator`; d\u00e9bito vis\u00edvel no HUD de mcoCoins;\ncluster do n\u00f3 mostra o card com a imagem + `mco_charged`; row em `generations` (UUID cit\u00e1vel) com\n`status='done'` e `mcoin_transactions` batendo; Vision-QA APROVADO no print 1920\u00d71080.\n"
}
```

```json
  "content": "-- Migration: 20260702190000_spaces_generations_ledger.sql\n-- Spaces Fase 1b (execution slice): `generations` ledger + atomic money RPCs.\n-- BoK SSOT: docs/bok/spaces-evolution/06-data-model.md \u00a7\"generations\" stub (:129-150) \u2014 columns/constraints\n--           copied verbatim; hardening divergences D1/D2/D3/D8 declared below (canonical security pattern wins).\n-- SOP (Lei 2, written BEFORE this code): docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md (decis\u00f5es S1-S13).\n--\n-- Debit order is LEDGER-FIRST (S1): begin_space_generation INSERTs the 'running' row AND debits in the SAME\n-- transaction (deduct RAISE \u21d2 rollback, never an orphan row) \u2014 resolves the BoK BPMN's internal contradiction\n-- (insert-after-provider vs the >30min stuck-run sweep needing a 'running' row, FMEA-SPACES-009).\n-- Idempotency lives in node_run_id UNIQUE + ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING (S2) \u2014 deduct_mco_coins has no idempotency key.\n--\n-- Tenancy: SELECT-own only; NO INSERT/UPDATE policy for authenticated (writes are service-role via the RPCs \u2014\n-- mirrors vision_jobs 20260615160000). RESTRICTIVE no-delete (financial-family convention, 20260624140000).\n-- Composite tenant-scoped FK (space_id, owner_id) \u2192 spaces(id, owner_id): a generation can NEVER point at\n-- another tenant's space (pattern 20260620120100).\n--\n-- Declared divergences from the BoK stub (SOP S-table):\n--   D1: + updated_at + shared trigger (SOL-SPACES-002 stuck-run detection is unsound on created_at alone).\n--   D2: + RESTRICTIVE no-delete policy (client DELETE blocked; FK CASCADE on space delete still works \u2014\n--       referential actions bypass RLS; the money trail lives in mcoin_transactions anyway).\n--   D3: + UNIQUE (id, owner_id) anchor on spaces + composite FK.\n--   D8: + result jsonb (nullable) \u2014 text outputs (magic_prompt) need a home for cluster preview + downstream\n--       threading (S11); precedent vision_jobs.result. Images live in creative_assets via asset_id.\n--   D6 note: mco columns are numeric (BoK semantics); all live costs are integers \u2014 the ledger's ::integer\n--       cast in deduct_mco_coins is latent-only for fractional amounts.\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 D3 anchor: composite-FK target on spaces \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nALTER TABLE public.spaces ADD CONSTRAINT spaces_id_owner_unique UNIQUE (id, owner_id);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 generations table (BoK stub verbatim + D1/D8) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE TABLE public.generations (\n  id           uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  owner_id     uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  space_id     uuid        NOT NULL,\n  node_id      text        NOT NULL,\n  node_run_id  uuid        NOT NULL UNIQUE,          -- idempot\u00eancia (BR \u00a76 / S2)\n  output_type  text        NOT NULL CHECK (output_type IN ('image','text','video','audio','svg','3d')),\n  asset_id     uuid        REFERENCES public.creative_assets(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n  model_key    text        NOT NULL,                 -- resolved provider/model (server truth \u2014 S8)\n  mco_charged  numeric     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (mco_charged >= 0),\n  mco_refunded numeric     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (mco_refunded >= 0 AND mco_refunded <= mco_charged), -- anti-mint\n  usd_cost     numeric,\n  latency_ms   integer,\n  result       jsonb,                                -- D8: small outputs (e.g. enhanced_prompt text)\n  status       text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'\n                 CHECK (status IN ('idle','pending','running','done','error','blocked')),\n  created_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),   -- D1\n  CONSTRAINT generations_space_tenant_fk FOREIGN KEY (space_id, owner_id)\n    REFERENCES public.spaces(id, owner_id) ON DELETE CASCADE   -- D3: tenant-scoped\n);\n\nALTER TABLE public.generations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;   -- default deny\n\n-- SELECT own only; NO write policies for authenticated (service-role RPCs are the only writers).\nCREATE POLICY \"generations: select own\" ON public.generations\n  FOR SELECT USING (owner_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- D2: financial-family no-delete (blocks client DELETE even if a permissive policy ever appears).\nCREATE POLICY \"generations: no delete\" ON public.generations\n  AS RESTRICTIVE FOR DELETE USING (false);\n\nCREATE INDEX generations_node_created_idx ON public.generations (node_id, created_at DESC); -- cluster cursor\nCREATE INDEX generations_space_idx        ON public.generations (space_id);\n-- Stuck-run sweep (self-heal-spaces.sh): running rows by age.\nCREATE INDEX generations_status_updated_idx ON public.generations (status, updated_at)\n  WHERE status IN ('pending','running');\n\nCREATE TRIGGER set_generations_updated_at\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.generations\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();\n\nCOMMENT ON TABLE public.generations IS\n  'spaces-evolution Fase 1b: execution ledger (1 row per node run \u2014 FR-SPACES-012). Ledger-first: row+debit in '\n  'one tx via begin_space_generation; refund via finalize_space_generation (claim status IN pending/running, '\n  'first finalizer wins). SELECT-own RLS; service-role writes only. SOP docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md.';\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 begin_space_generation \u2014 atomic ledger-first debit (S1/S2) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n-- INSERT the 'running' row keyed on node_run_id; conflict \u21d2 idempotent no-op (returns prior state, ZERO second\n-- debit). Then debits in the SAME tx (deduct RAISE \u21d2 full rollback \u2014 no orphan row, no charge-without-row).\n-- p_amount 0 (future no-op runs) skips the deduct. Caller (canvas-execute Spaces branch) pre-checks 402s;\n-- the RPC is the race-safe authority. service_role-only via grants (drift guard: audit-privileged-rpc-grants.sh).\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.begin_space_generation(\n  p_owner_id    uuid,\n  p_space_id    uuid,\n  p_node_id     text,\n  p_node_run_id uuid,\n  p_model_key   text,\n  p_output_type text,\n  p_amount      numeric,\n  p_context     jsonb DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb\n)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = ''\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  v_id     uuid;\n  v_dup_id uuid;\n  v_dup_st text;\nBEGIN\n  IF p_amount IS NULL OR p_amount < 0 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'begin_space_generation: p_amount must be >= 0' USING errcode = '22023';\n  END IF;\n\n  INSERT INTO public.generations (owner_id, space_id, node_id, node_run_id, output_type, model_key, mco_charged, status)\n  VALUES (p_owner_id, p_space_id, p_node_id, p_node_run_id, p_output_type, p_model_key, p_amount, 'running')\n  ON CONFLICT (node_run_id) DO NOTHING\n  RETURNING id INTO v_id;\n\n  IF v_id IS NULL THEN\n    -- Duplicate node_run_id \u21d2 a prior attempt already begun/charged. Return its state; NEVER debit again (S2/G2).\n    SELECT id, status INTO v_dup_id, v_dup_st FROM public.generations WHERE node_run_id = p_node_run_id;\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'duplicate', true, 'generation_id', v_dup_id, 'status', v_dup_st);\n  END IF;\n\n  IF p_amount > 0 THEN\n    -- Same-tx debit: RAISE (insufficient balance / frozen account) rolls the INSERT back.\n    PERFORM public.deduct_mco_coins(\n      p_owner_id, p_amount, 'spaces.node.run',\n      p_context || jsonb_build_object('node_run_id', p_node_run_id, 'space_id', p_space_id, 'generation_id', v_id)\n    );\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'duplicate', false, 'generation_id', v_id);\nEND;\n$$;\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 finalize_space_generation \u2014 claimed terminal transition + idempotent refund (S6) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n-- Guard `status IN ('pending','running')` \u21d2 exactly the FIRST finalizer wins (edge fn OR the stuck-run sweep);\n-- a second call is a no-op (no double refund). Refund clamped 0 \u2264 refund \u2264 mco_charged (CHECK is the backstop).\n-- uid ALWAYS from the claimed row (tenant safety). Refund credit (add_mco_coins) + symmetric mcoin_transactions\n-- row (action 'spaces.node.refund' \u2014 reconciliation key KPI-SPACES-004) commit in the SAME transaction.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_space_generation(\n  p_node_run_id uuid,\n  p_status      text,\n  p_refund      numeric DEFAULT 0,\n  p_asset_id    uuid    DEFAULT NULL,\n  p_result      jsonb   DEFAULT NULL,\n  p_usd_cost    numeric DEFAULT NULL,\n  p_latency_ms  integer DEFAULT NULL\n)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = ''\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  v_uid    uuid;\n  v_refund numeric;\nBEGIN\n  IF p_status NOT IN ('done','error') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'finalize_space_generation: terminal status required, got %', p_status USING errcode = '22023';\n  END IF;\n\n  UPDATE public.generations\n     SET status       = p_status,\n         asset_id     = COALESCE(p_asset_id, asset_id),\n         result       = COALESCE(p_result, result),\n         usd_cost     = COALESCE(p_usd_cost, usd_cost),\n         latency_ms   = COALESCE(p_latency_ms, latency_ms),\n         mco_refunded = LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(p_refund, 0), 0), mco_charged)\n   WHERE node_run_id = p_node_run_id\n     AND status IN ('pending','running')\n   RETURNING owner_id, mco_refunded INTO v_uid, v_refund;\n\n  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', false);   -- already terminal \u2192 no-op (no double refund)\n  END IF;\n\n  IF v_refund > 0 THEN\n    -- Positive credit (NEVER a negative deduct \u2014 anti-mint 20260603220000 lesson). ::integer matches\n    -- add_mco_coins(uuid, integer); all live costs are integers (D6 note).\n    PERFORM public.add_mco_coins(v_uid, v_refund::integer);\n    INSERT INTO public.mcoin_transactions (user_id, action, amount, context)\n    VALUES (v_uid, 'spaces.node.refund', v_refund::integer,\n            jsonb_build_object('node_run_id', p_node_run_id, 'status', p_status));\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', true, 'refunded', v_refund);\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('finalized', true, 'refunded', 0);\nEND;\n$$;\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 Grants: service-role-only (same-migration trailer \u2014 grant-drift lesson 20260701030000) \u2500\u2500\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.begin_space_generation(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, text, text, numeric, jsonb)\n  FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.begin_space_generation(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, text, text, numeric, jsonb)\n  TO service_role;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.finalize_space_generation(uuid, text, numeric, uuid, jsonb, numeric, integer)\n  FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.finalize_space_generation(uuid, text, numeric, uuid, jsonb, numeric, integer)\n  TO service_role;\n"
}
```

const t0 = Date.now();\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 1b slice gate (SOP S7): only these node_types execute in Spaces mode \u2500\u2500\n  if (body.node_type !== \"generate_image\" && body.node_type !== }\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 422 guards BEFORE any debit (gate G4) \u2500\u2500\n  if (body.node_type === \"generate_image\") {\n    const pair = `${body.provider}/${body.model}`;\n    if model_requested: pair,\n        allowed: [...SPACES_IMAGE_MODELS],\n      }, corsHeaders);\n    }\n    if (isImagePromptTooLong(body.prompt)) {\n      return spacesJson(422, {\n        error: }, corsHeaders);\n  }\n  if (begun?.duplicate) {\n    // Idempotent replay (SOP S2): prior attempt already charged \u2014 return its state, ZERO second debit.\n    return spacesJson(200, {\n attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {\n    try {\n      provErr = \"\";\n      if (body.node_type === \"magic_prompt\") {\n        enhancedPrompt = await generateMagicPrompt(\n          keys.openrouter, keys.gemini, body.prompt,\n          body.parameters?.level || \"curto\",\n          body.parameters?.target_model || \"generic\",\n          \"Nenhum contexto de campanha dispon\u00edvel.\",\n        );\n      } else if (body.provider === \"openrouter\") {\n        const r = await generateOpenRouter(keys.openrouter, body);\n        outputUrl = r.url; outputB64 = r.b64;\n      } else {\n        // Unreachable behind SPACES_IMAGE_MODELS (both pairs are openrouter) \u2014 kept as a hard stop.\n        throw new Error(`Unknown Spaces provider: ${body.provider}`);\n      }\n      break;\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      provErr = e?.message || \"Provider error\";\n      console.error(`[canvas-execute:spaces] provider attempt ${attempt + 1} failed:`, provErr);\n      if (attempt === 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000));\n    }\n  }\n\n  const producedNothing = body.node_type === \"magic_prompt\"\n    ? !enhancedPrompt\n    : (!outputUrl && !outputB64);\n\n  if (provErr || producedNothing) {\n    // \u2500\u2500 finalize error + FULL refund in the SAME error flow (FMEA-SPACES-001 / SOP S6) \u2500\u2500\n    const { data: fin, error: finErr } = await admin.rpc(\"finalize_space_generation\", {\n      p_node_run_id: body.node_run_id,\n      p_status: \"error\",\n      p_refund: creditCost,\n      p_latency_ms: Date.now() - t0,\n    });\n    if (finErr) console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] finalize(error) failed:\", finErr);\n    await admin.from(\"infra_health_logs\").insert({\n      service: \"canvas-execute\", console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] health log failed:\", e));\n    return spacesJson(502, {\n      error: \"provider_failed\",\n      message: \"Falha do provedor de gera\u00e7\u00e3o \u2014 o valor debitado foi estornado.\",\n      execution_id: generationId,\n      refunded: fin?.refunded ?? creditCost,\n      detail: provErr || \"empty output\",\n    }, corsHeaders);\n  }\n\n  //
