body.space_id!);\n    return spacesJson(200, {\n      execution_id: generationId,\n      status: \"done\",\n      enhanced_prompt: enhancedPrompt,\n      credits_charged: creditCost,\n    }, corsHeaders);\n  }\n\n  // Image: persist bytes to the canvas-assets bucket (provider URLs have short TTLs \u2014 legacy pattern).\n  let storedUrl = outputUrl;\n  let storedKey: string | null = null;\n  let storedContentType = \"image/png\";\n  try {\n    let bytes: Uint8Array | null = null;\n    if (outputB64) {\n      bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(outputB64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));\n    } else if (outputUrl) {\n      const dl = await fetch(outputUrl);\n      if (dl.ok) {\n        const ct = dl.headers.get(\"content-type\") || \"\";\n        if (ct.startsWith(\"image/\")) storedContentType = ct;\n        bytes = new Uint8Array(await dl.arrayBuffer());\n      }\n    }\n    if (bytes && bytes.byteLength > 0) {\n      const ext = storedContentType.split(\"/\")[1]?.split(\";\")[0] || \"png\";\n      const fileName = `${user.id}/spaces/${generationId}.${ext}`;\n      const { error: upErr } = await admin.storage\n        .from(\"canvas-assets\").upload(fileName, bytes, { contentType: storedContentType, upsert: true });\n      if (!upErr) {\n        const { data: urlData } = admin.storage.from(\"canvas-assets\").getPublicUrl(fileName);\n        storedUrl = urlData.publicUrl;\n        storedKey = fileName;\n      } else {\n        console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] upload error:\", upErr);\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] upload error:\", e);\n  }\n\n  if (!storedUrl) {\n    // Bytes existed but neither re-host nor provider URL is usable \u2014 no value delivered \u21d2 refund.\n    await admin.rpc(\"finalize_space_generation\", {\n      p_node_run_id: body.node_run_id, p_status: \"error\", p_refund: creditCost,\n      p_latency_ms: Date.now() - t0,\n    });\n    return spacesJson(502, {\n      error: \"asset_persist_failed\",\n      message: \"Falha ao materializar a imagem \u2014 o valor debitado foi estornado.\",\n      execution_id: generationId,\n      refunded: creditCost,\n    }, corsHeaders);\n  }\n\n  // Canonical asset registry (bidirectional spine) \u2014 returns the creative_assets uuid. Fail-soft.\n  let assetId: string | null = null;\n  if (storedKey) {\n    try {\n      const { data: regId } = await admin.rpc(\"register_creative_asset\", {\n        p_user_id: user.id,\n        p_kind: \"image\",\n        p_storage_bucket: \"canvas-assets\",\n        p_storage_key: storedKey,\n        p_is_public: true,\n        p_source_module: \"spaces\",\n        p_source_job_id: generationId,\n        p_mime_type: storedContentType,\n        p_provider: body.provider,\n        p_model: body.model,\n        p_prompt: body.prompt,\n      });\n      assetId = (regId as string) ?? null;\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] creative_assets register skipped:\", e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  await admin.rpc(\"finalize_space_generation\", {\n    p_node_run_id: body.node_run_id,\n    p_status: \"done\",\n    p_refund: 0,\n    p_asset_id: assetId,\n    p_result: { output_url: storedUrl },\n    p_latency_ms: Date.now() - t0,\n  });\n  await spacesFirstRunObservation(admin, user.id, body.space_id!);\n\n  return spacesJson(200, {\n    execution_id: generationId,\n    status: \"done\",\n    output_url: storedUrl,\n    asset_id: assetId,\n    credits_charged: creditCost,\n  }, corsHeaders);\n}\n\n/** PROC-SPACES-008 \u2014 first `done` generation of a Space seeds a Knowledge Mesh observation node.\n *  Fail-open: NEVER blocks or fails the run (FMEA-SPACES-010). */\nasync function spacesFirstRunObservation(admin: any, userId: string, spaceId: string) {\n  try {\n    const { count } = await admin\n      .from(\"generations\")\n      .select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n      .eq(\"space_id\", spaceId)\n      .eq(\"status\", \"done\");\n    if ((count ?? 0) !== 1) return;\n    await admin.from(\"mcorch_nodes\").insert({\n      user_id: userId,\n      node_type: \"observation\",\n      name: `spaces-first-run-${spaceId}`,\n      content: `Primeira gera\u00e7\u00e3o conclu\u00edda no Space ${spaceId} (Spaces Fase 1b \u2014 canvas\u2192conte\u00fado pago vivo).`,\n      metadata: { provider: \"spaces\", space_id: spaceId, event: \"spaces_first_run\" },\n      revenue_impact: 0,\n      stability_score: 0.85,\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] first-run observation skipped (fail-open):\", e);\n  }\n}\n\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\n// PROVIDER IMPLEMENTATIONS\n// \u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550"

> ```
> 1	// Shared helper for Supabase Edge Function invocations from the client.
> 2	// supabase.functions.invoke() returns a FunctionsHttpError for any non-2xx response, with the raw
> 3	// Response on `error.context`. Our edge fns return a PT-BR `{ error }` body (e.g. 402 "Saldo insuficiente"),
> 4	// so we read that to surface the real message to the user instead of the generic "Edge Function returned a
> 5	// non-2xx status code".
> 6	
> 7	/** Extract a PT-BR error message from a Supabase Edge Function non-2xx response. */
> 8	export async function edgeErrorMessage(error: unknown, fallback: string): Promise<string> {
> 9	  const ctx = (error as { context?: { json?: () => Promise<{ error?: string; action?: string }> } })?.context;
> 10	  try {
> 11	    const body = await ctx?.json?.();
> 12	    // Prefer the human-readable `action` hint (e.g. "Configure suas credenciais de app TikTok em
> 13	    // /dashboard/settings") over the machine code in `error` (e.g. "tiktok_not_configured").
> 14	    if (body?.action) return body.action;
> 15	    if (body?.error) return body.error;
> 16	  } catch { /* response body was not JSON */ }
> 17	  return (error as { message?: string })?.message || fallback;
> 18	}
> 19	
> ```

Fase 1a nodes don't persist `typeId` \u2014 fall back to the\n * label prefix (\"image-generator #1\" \u2192 \"image-generator\"), which addNode has always minted.\n */\nexport function nodeTypeId(node: SpaceNode): string | null {\n  const explicit = node.data.typeId;\n  if (explicit && NODE_TYPE_IDS.includes(explicit)) return explicit;\n  const prefix = (node.data.label || '').replace(/\\s*#\\d+\\s*$/, '').trim();\n  return NODE_TYPE_IDS.includes(prefix) ? prefix : null;\n}\n\nexport function classifyNode(node: SpaceNode): NodeExecClass {\n  const t = nodeTypeId(node);\n  if (t && EXECUTABLE_TYPES.has(t)) return 'executable';\n  if (t && PASSIVE_TYPES.has(t)) return 'passive';\n  return 'unsupported'; // includes image-upscaler (cut from 1b \u2014 SOP S7) and all video/audio/svg/designer\n}\n\n/* \u2500\u2500 S8 \u2014 model resolution (Spaces key \u2192 server-supported provider/model) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */\nexport interface ResolvedModel { provider: string; model: string }\n\nexport const SPACES_MODEL_MAP: Record<string, ResolvedModel> = {\n  'auto': { provider: 'openrouter', model: 'google/gemini-2.5-flash-image' },\n  'flux.1.1': { provider: 'openrouter', model: 'black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro' },\n};\n\n/** Mirror of the server CREDIT_COSTS for the RESOLVED pairs (mirror-parity test G7). */\nexport const SPACES_EXECUTABLE_COSTS: Record<string, number> = {\n  'auto': 10,      // openrouter/google/gemini-2.5-flash-image\n  'flux.1.1': 12,  // openrouter/black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro\n};\n\n/** Unknown Spaces model key resolves as 'auto' (Pattern #2 minimal routing \u2014 SOP S8). */\nexport function resolveSpacesModel(modelKey?: string): ResolvedModel {\n  return SPACES_MODEL_MAP[modelKey ?? 'auto'] ?? SPACES_MODEL_MAP['auto'];\n}\n\n/** Estimate = real charge for the executable slice (gate G7 \u2014 HUD honesty). */\nexport function estimateExecutableCost(node: SpaceNode): number {\n  const t = nodeTypeId(node);\n  if (t === 'prompt-generator') {\n    const level = (node.data.params?.level as string) || 'curto';\n    return level === 'longo' ? 5 : level === 'medio' ? 2 : 1; // mirrors magic_prompt server pricing\n  }\n  if (t === 'image-generator') {\n    const base = SPACES_EXECUTABLE_COSTS[node.data.modelKey ?? 'auto'] ?? SPACES_EXECUTABLE_COSTS['auto'];\n    return base * Math.max(1, node.data.batch ?? 1); // S10: batch N = N independent runs\n  }\n  return 0;\n}\n\nexport function projectSpacesGraphCost(nodes: SpaceNode[]): number {\n  return nodes\n    .filter((n) => classifyNode(n) === 'executable')\n    .reduce((sum, n) => sum + estimateExecutableCost(n), 0);\n}\n\n/* \u2500\u2500 Partial failure \u2014 BFS over outgoing edges (FR-SPACES-005) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */\nexport function collectDescendants(nodeId: string, edges: SpaceEdge[]): Set<string> {\n  const out = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  for (const e of edges) {\n    if (!out.has(e.source)) out.set(e.source, []);\n    out.get(e.source)!.push(e.target);\n  }\n  const blocked = new Set<string>();\n  const queue = [...(out.get(nodeId) ?? [])];\n  while (queue.length) {\n    const id = queue.shift()!;\n    if (blocked.has(id)) continue;\n    blocked.add(id);\n    queue.push(...(out.get(id) ?? []));\n  }\n  return blocked;\n}\n\n/* \u2500\u2500 S11 \u2014 input threading (single-output convention, 1b) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */\nexport interface NodeOutput { type: 'text' | 'image'; value: string }\nexport interface UpstreamInputs { promptText?: string; referenceImages: string[] }\n\n/**\n * Resolve a node's inputs from upstream outputs: edge into handle `prompt` + text output \u2192 prompt;\n * edge into handle `reference` + image output \u2192 reference image. Tolerates untyped hydrated edges.\n */\nexport function resolveUpstreamInputs(\n  node: SpaceNode,\n  edges: SpaceEdge[],\n  outputs: Map<string, NodeOutput>,\n): UpstreamInputs {\n  const result: UpstreamInputs = { referenceImages: [] };\n  for (const e of edges) {\n    if (e.target !== node.id) continue;\n    const up = outputs.get(e.source);\n    if (!up) continue;\n    if (e.targetHandle === 'prompt' && up.type === 'text') result.promptText = up.value;\n    if (e.targetHandle === 'reference' && up.type === 'image') result.referenceImages.push(up.value);\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n\n/* \u2500\u2500 Payload builder (S4 additive extension of the live canvas-execute shape) \u2500 */\nexport interface SpacesExecutePayload {\n  project_id: string;      // legacy-required field \u2014 carries the spaceId\n  node_id: string;\n  node_type: 'generate_image' | 'magic_prompt';\n  provider: string;\n  model: string;\n  prompt: string;\n  parameters: Record<string, unknown>;\n  reference_image_urls?: string[];\n  space_id: string;        // ledger-mode trigger (with node_run_id)\n  node_run_id: string;\n}\n\n/** Returns null when the node has no usable prompt (skipped as no-op \u2014 never charged). */\nexport function buildExecutePayload(\n  node: SpaceNode,\n  spaceId: string,\n  nodeRunId: string,\n  upstream: UpstreamInputs,\n): SpacesExecutePayload | null {\n  const t = nodeTypeId(node);\n  const ownPrompt = ((node.data.params?.prompt as string) || '').trim();\n  const prompt = (upstream.promptText || ownPrompt).trim();\n  if (!prompt) return null;\n\n  if (t === 'prompt-generator') {\n    return {\n      project_id: spaceId, node_id: node.id, node_type: 'magic_prompt',\n      provider: 'openrouter', model: 'magic_prompt', prompt,\n      parameters: { level: (node.data.params?.level as string) || 'curto' },\n      space_id: spaceId, node_run_id: nodeRunId,\n    };\n  }\n  if (t === 'image-generator') {\n    const { provider, model } = resolveSpacesModel(node.data.modelKey);\n    return {\n      project_id: spaceId, node_id: node.id, node_type: 'generate_image',\n      provider, model, prompt,\n      parameters: { aspect_ratio: node.data.aspectRatio ?? '1:1' },\n      ...(upstream.referenceImages.length ? { reference_image_urls: upstream.referenceImages } : {}),\n      space_id: spaceId, node_run_id: nodeRunId,\n    };\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/* \u2500\u2500 Row \u2192 cluster-card mapper (C9 \u2014 zod card shape; server CHECKs own invariants) \u2500 */\nexport interface GenerationRow {\n  id: string; space_id: string; node_id: string; node_run_id: string;\n  output_type: string; asset_id: string | null; model_key: string;\n  mco_charged: number; mco_refunded: number; usd_cost: number | null;\n  latency_ms: number | null; result: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n  status: string; created_at: string;\n}\n\nexport function rowToGeneration(row: GenerationRow): Generation & { previewUrl?: string; previewText?: string } {\n  return {\n    id: row.id,\n    spaceId: row.space_id,\n    nodeId: row.node_id,\n    outputType: row.output_type as Generation['outputType'],\n    assetId: row.asset_id ?? undefined,\n    modelKey: row.model_key,\n    mcoCharged: Number(row.mco_charged) || 0,\n    mcoRefunded: Number(row.mco_refunded) || 0,\n    usdCost: row.usd_cost != null ? Number(row.usd_cost) : undefined,\n    latencyMs: row.latency_ms ?? undefined,\n    status: row.status as Generation['status'],\n    createdAt: row.created_at,\n    previewUrl: (row.result?.output_url as string) || undefined,\n    previewText: (row.result?.enhanced_prompt as string) || undefined,\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "/** Strip xyflow-volatile fields before persisting (selection/drag state is session-local).\n *  Transient run statuses (pending/running) reset to idle (SOP spaces-graph-execution S9) \u2014\n *  a reload must never hydrate a ghost in-flight run; done/error/blocked persist as history. */\nfunction serializeGraph(s: SpaceGraphSnapshot) {\n  return {\n    nodes: s.nodes.map((n) => ({\n      ...n, selected: undefined, dragging: undefined, measured: undefined,\n      data: (n.data.status === 'pending' || n.data.status === 'running')\n        ? { ...n.data, status: 'idle' as const }\n        : n.data,\n    })),\n    edges: s.edges.map((e) => ({ ...e, selected: undefined })),\n    viewport: s.viewport,\n  };\n}"
}

  "new_string": "/** Estimate mcoCoins for a single node run (display only; server reconciles).\n *  Fase 1b (gate G7 \u2014 SOP spaces-graph-execution S8): executable nodes estimate via the\n *  1b executor map so estimate = real charge; non-executable nodes cost 0 (never run). */\nexport function estimateCost(node: SpaceNode): number {\n  if (node.data.byok) return 0; // BR-SPACES-006 (dormant in 1b \u2014 SOP S12)\n  if (classifyNode(node) !== 'executable') return 0;\n  return estimateExecutableCost(node);\n}\n\n/** Project total cost for a set of nodes (pre-run \u2014 BR-SPACES-008 / dry-run BR-SPACES-005). */\nexport function projectGraphCost(nodes: SpaceNode[]): number {\n  return nodes.reduce((sum, n) => sum + estimateCost(n), 0);\n}"
}

{

  "content": "// useSpaceSession \u2014 FR-SPACES-006 session gate (SD-SPACES-004). ensureFresh() is called before any\n// paid run: near-expiry/absent session gets ONE refresh attempt; failure aborts the run client-side.\n// The server is ALWAYS the authority (canvas-execute revalidates the JWT \u2014 this is UX, not security).\nimport { useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\n\nconst FRESHNESS_MARGIN_MS = 60_000;\n\nexport function useSpaceSession() {\n  const ensureFresh = useCallback(async (): Promise<boolean> => {\n    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\n    const expMs = (data.session?.expires_at ?? 0) * 1000;\n    if (data.session && expMs - Date.now() > FRESHNESS_MARGIN_MS) return true;\n    const { data: refreshed, error } = await supabase.auth.refreshSession();\n    return !error && !!refreshed.session;\n  }, []);\n\n  return { ensureFresh };\n}\n"
}

  "content": "// useGenerationLedger \u2014 the ONLY client surface of the Spaces execution ledger (SD-SPACES-003).\n// estimate (dry-run, zero server calls \u2014 BR-SPACES-005) \u00b7 runGraph (FR-SPACES-005, layer-by-layer via\n// canvas-execute ledger mode) \u00b7 cluster reads (FR-SPACES-012, lazy \u226424/page). ALL money moves happen\n// server-side (begin/finalize RPCs) \u2014 this hook never touches balances.\n// SOP: docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md.\nimport { useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { useInfiniteQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\nimport { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\nimport { useSpacesStore } from '@/store/useSpacesStore';\nimport { getSpaceLayers } from '@/lib/spaces-dag';\nimport {\n  classifyNode, buildExecutePayload, resolveUpstreamInputs, collectDescendants,\n  projectSpacesGraphCost, rowToGeneration,\n  type NodeOutput, type GenerationRow,\n} from '@/lib/spaces-execution';\nimport { useSpaceSession } from '@/hooks/useSpaceSession';\nimport { toast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';\nimport type { SpaceNode } from '@/lib/spaces-schema';\n\nconst PAGE_SIZE = 24;\n\ninterface ExecResponse {\n  execution_id: string;\n  status: string;\n  output_url?: string;\n  enhanced_prompt?: string;\n  duplicate?: boolean;\n  credits_charged: number;\n}\n\n/** Structured run failure \u2014 kind drives node status + run control (FRD error table 04:100-107). */\nclass RunNodeError extends Error {\n  kind: 'blocked' | 'error' | 'abort';\n  constructor(kind: 'blocked' | 'error' | 'abort', message: string) {\n    super(message);\n    this.kind = kind;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Extract {status, code, message} from a FunctionsHttpError and map to run semantics. */\nasync function toRunError(error: unknown): Promise<RunNodeError> {\n  const ctx = (error as { context?: Response })?.context;\n  let status = 0;\n  let code = '';\n  let message = '';\n  try {\n    status = (ctx as Response | undefined)?.status ?? 0;\n    const body = await (ctx as Response | undefined)?.json?.();\n    code = (body?.error as string) ?? '';\n    message = (body?.message as string) ?? code;\n  } catch { /* body was not JSON */ }\n\n  if (status === 402) {\n    const daily = /daily|cap/i.test(code);\n    return new RunNodeError('blocked', daily\n      ? 'Teto di\u00e1rio de mcoCoins do dia j\u00e1 atingido.'\n      : 'Saldo de mcoCoins insuficiente.');\n  }\n  if (status === 404) return new RunNodeError('abort', 'Space n\u00e3o encontrado.');\n  if (status === 422) return new RunNodeError('error', message || 'Par\u00e2metros inv\u00e1lidos para este n\u00f3.');\n  if (status === 502) return new RunNodeError('error', message || 'Falha do provedor \u2014 o valor debitado foi estornado.');\n  return new RunNodeError('error', message || (error as Error)?.message || 'Falha na execu\u00e7\u00e3o do n\u00f3.');\n}\n\n/** Seed the in-run output map with each node's LATEST done generation (S11 threading across runs). */\nasync function seedOutputs(spaceId: string, outputs: Map<string, NodeOutput>) {\n  const { data } = await supabase\n    .from('generations')\n    .select('node_id, output_type, result, created_at')\n    .eq('space_id', spaceId)\n    .eq('status', 'done')\n    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n    .limit(200);\n  for (const row of data ?? []) {\n    if (outputs.has(row.node_id)) continue; // newest wins (ordered DESC)\n    const result = (row.result ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;\n    if (row.output_type === 'text' && typeof result.enhanced_prompt === 'string') {\n      outputs.set(row.node_id, { type: 'text', value: result.enhanced_prompt });\n    } else if (row.output_type === 'image' && typeof result.output_url === 'string') {\n      outputs.set(row.node_id, { type: 'image', value: result.output_url });\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nexport function useGenerationLedger(spaceId?: string) {\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\n  const { ensureFresh } = useSpaceSession();\n\n  /** Dry-run projection (BR-SPACES-005/008) \u2014 pure client math, zero d\u00e9bito, zero server calls. */\n  const estimate = useCallback(\n    () => projectSpacesGraphCost(useSpacesStore.getState().nodes),\n    [],\n  );\n\n  const runGraph = useCallback(async () => {\n    if (!spaceId) return;\n    const store = useSpacesStore.getState();\n    if (store.runState === 'running') return;\n\n    if (!(await ensureFresh())) {\n      toast({ title: 'Sess\u00e3o expirada \u2014 fa\u00e7a login novamente.', variant: 'destructive' });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    let layers: SpaceNode[][];\n    try {\n      layers = getSpaceLayers(store.nodes, store.edges);\n    } catch {\n      toast({ title: 'Ciclo detectado no grafo \u2014 remova a conex\u00e3o circular.', variant: 'destructive' });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const executables = store.nodes.filter((n) => classifyNode(n) === 'executable');\n    if (executables.length === 0) {\n      toast({ title: 'Nenhum n\u00f3 execut\u00e1vel na Fase 1', description: 'Adicione um Gerador de imagem ou Assistant.' });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    store.setRunState('running');\n    const outputs = new Map<string, NodeOutput>();\n    try {\n      await seedOutputs(spaceId, outputs);\n    } catch { /* seeding is best-effort \u2014 in-run outputs still thread */ }\n\n    const blockedIds = new Set<string>();\n    let hadError = false;\n    let ranAny = false;\n    let aborted = false;\n    let warnedUnsupported = false;\n\n    const runNode = async (node: SpaceNode) => {\n      const st = useSpacesStore.getState();\n      const upstream = resolveUpstreamInputs(node, st.edges, outputs);\n      const batch = Math.max(1, node.data.batch ?? 1);\n      st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'running');\n      for (let i = 0; i < batch; i++) {\n        // S3: node_run_id minted ONCE per attempt (idempotency anchor server-side).\n        const nodeRunId = crypto.randomUUID();\n        const payload = buildExecutePayload(node, spaceId, nodeRunId, upstream);\n        if (!payload) {\n          st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'idle');\n          toast({ title: `\"${node.data.label}\" sem prompt \u2014 pulado (nada cobrado).` });\n          return;\n        }\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('canvas-execute', { body: payload });\n        if (error) throw await toRunError(error);\n        const res = data as ExecResponse;\n        ranAny = true;\n        if (res.enhanced_prompt) outputs.set(node.id, { type: 'text', value: res.enhanced_prompt });\n        else if (res.output_url) outputs.set(node.id, { type: 'image', value: res.output_url });\n      }\n      st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'done');\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['generations', spaceId, node.id] });\n    };\n\n    for (const layer of layers) {\n      if (aborted) break;\n\n      const unsupported = layer.filter((n) => classifyNode(n) === 'unsupported');\n      if (unsupported.length > 0 && !warnedUnsupported) {\n        warnedUnsupported = true;\n        toast({\n          title: 'Alguns n\u00f3s n\u00e3o s\u00e3o suportados na Fase 1',\n          description: 'Eles permanecem como est\u00e3o \u2014 nada \u00e9 cobrado por eles.',\n        });\n      }\n\n      const toRun = layer.filter((n) => classifyNode(n) === 'executable' && !blockedIds.has(n.id));\n      if (toRun.length === 0) continue;\n\n      const results = await Promise.allSettled(toRun.map((n) => runNode(n)));\n      results.forEach((r, i) => {\n        if (r.status !== 'rejected') return;\n        const node = toRun[i];\n        const err = r.reason instanceof RunNodeError ? r.reason : new RunNodeError('error', String(r.reason));\n        hadError = true;\n        const st = useSpacesStore.getState();\n        if (err.kind === 'blocked' || err.kind === 'abort') {\n          st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'blocked');\n          aborted = true; // 402/404: no point continuing \u2014 remaining nodes stay untouched\n        } else {\n          st.updateNodeStatus(node.id, 'error');\n          for (const descId of collectDescendants(node.id, st.edges)) {\n            blockedIds.add(descId);\n            if (st.nodes.some((n) => n.id === descId && classifyNode(n) === 'executable')) {\n              st.updateNodeStatus(descId, 'blocked');\n            }\n          }\n        }\n        toast({ title: `\"${node.data.label}\" falhou`, description: err.message, variant: 'destructive' });\n      });\n    }\n\n    useSpacesStore.getState().setRunState(hadError ? 'partial' : ranAny ? 'done' : 'idle');\n  }, [spaceId, ensureFresh, qc]);\n\n  return { estimate, runGraph };\n}\n\n/** Cluster reader (FR-SPACES-012/002): lazy \u226424 cards/page, cursor = created_at DESC, staleTime 60s. */\nexport function useGenerationCluster(spaceId?: string, nodeId?: string | null) {\n  return useInfiniteQuery({\n    queryKey: ['generations', spaceId, nodeId],\n    enabled: !!spaceId && !!nodeId,\n    staleTime: 60_000,\n    initialPageParam: null as string | null,\n    queryFn: async ({ pageParam }) => {\n      let q = supabase\n        .from('generations')\n        .select('*')\n        .eq('space_id', spaceId!)\n        .eq('node_id', nodeId!)\n        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\n        .limit(PAGE_SIZE);\n      if (pageParam) q = q.lt('created_at', pageParam);\n      const { data, error } = await q;\n      if (error) throw error;\n      return ((data ?? []) as GenerationRow[]).map(rowToGeneration);\n    },\n    getNextPageParam: (last) => (last.length === PAGE_SIZE ? last[last.length - 1].createdAt : undefined),\n  });\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "// GenerationCluster \u2014 lazy generation history of the selected node (FR-SPACES-002/012/014).\n// Render-only: data via useGenerationCluster (TanStack InfiniteQuery); \u226424 cards/page, cursor\n// created_at DESC, IntersectionObserver sentinel, loading=\"lazy\" thumbs. Re-runs ADD, never overwrite.\nimport { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport { useGenerationCluster } from '@/hooks/useGenerationLedger';\nimport { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';\nimport type { NodeStatus } from '@/lib/spaces-schema';\n\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<NodeStatus, string> = {\n  idle: 'ocioso', pending: 'na fila', running: 'gerando', done: 'conclu\u00edda', error: 'falhou', blocked: 'bloqueada',\n};\nconst STATUS_CLASS: Record<NodeStatus, string> = {\n  idle: 'text-muted-foreground', pending: 'text-amber-400', running: 'text-blue-400',\n  done: 'text-emerald-400', error: 'text-red-400', blocked: 'text-orange-400',\n};\n\nexport function GenerationCluster({ spaceId, nodeId }: { spaceId: string; nodeId: string }) {\n  const { data, isLoading, isError, hasNextPage, isFetchingNextPage, fetchNextPage } =\n    useGenerationCluster(spaceId, nodeId);\n  const sentinelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const el = sentinelRef.current;\n    if (!el || !hasNextPage) return;\n    const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {\n      if (entries[0]?.isIntersecting && !isFetchingNextPage) fetchNextPage();\n    });\n    io.observe(el);\n    return () => io.disconnect();\n  }, [hasNextPage, isFetchingNextPage, fetchNextPage]);\n\n  const items = (data?.pages ?? []).flat();\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return <div className=\"flex justify-center py-4\"><Loader2 className=\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-muted-foreground\" /></div>;\n  }\n  if (isError) {\n    return <p className=\"text-xs text-destructive py-2\">Falha ao carregar as gera\u00e7\u00f5es \u2014 tente novamente.</p>;\n  }\n  if (items.length === 0) {\n    return <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground py-2\">Nenhuma gera\u00e7\u00e3o ainda \u2014 rode \u25b6 para gerar.</p>;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n      {items.map((g) => (\n        <div key={g.id} className=\"rounded-md border border-border bg-card/60 p-2 space-y-1.5\">\n          {g.previewUrl ? (\n            <img\n              src={g.previewUrl} alt={`Gera\u00e7\u00e3o ${g.modelKey}`} loading=\"lazy\"\n              className=\"w-full rounded max-h-40 object-cover\"\n            />\n          ) : g.previewText ? (\n            <p className=\"text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap line-clamp-4\">{g.previewText}</p>\n          ) : null}\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">\n            <span className=\"truncate max-w-[55%]\" title={g.modelKey}>{g.modelKey}</span>\n            <span className={STATUS_CLASS[g.status]}>{STATUS_LABEL[g.status]}</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">\n            <span>\n              {g.mcoCharged} mco\n              {(g.mcoRefunded ?? 0) > 0 && (\n                <span className=\"text-emerald-400\"> \u00b7 estorno {g.mcoRefunded}</span>\n              )}\n            </span>\n            <span>{new Date(g.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n      <div ref={sentinelRef} className=\"h-1\" />\n      {isFetchingNextPage && (\n        <div className=\"flex justify-center py-2\"><Loader2 className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin text-muted-foreground\" /></div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

'Rodando\u2026' : 'Rodar'}\n      </Button>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function SpaceCanvasPage() {\n  const { spaceId } = useParams<{ spaceId: string }>();\n  const { data: space, isLoading } = useSpace(spaceId);"

'n1',\n    type: 'spaceNode',\n    position: { x: 0, y: 0 },\n    data: {\n      label: partial.label ?? 'node',\n      typeId: partial.typeId,\n      category: 'image',\n      aspectRatio: '1:1',\n      batch: partial.batch ?? 1,\n      references: [],\n      coinCost: 0,\n      stabilityScore: 1,\n      status: 'idle',\n      ports: [],\n      params: partial.params ?? {},\n      icon: '\u2728',\n      modelKey: partial.modelKey,\n    },\n  } as SpaceNode;\n}\n\nfunction mkEdge(source: string, }))).toBe('executable');\n    expect(classifyNode(mkNode({ typeId: 'sticky-note' }))).toBe('passive');\n    expect(classifyNode(mkNode({ typeId: 'video-generator' }))).toBe('unsupported');\n    expect(classifyNode(mkNode({ typeId: 'image-upscaler' }))).toBe('unsupported'); // cut from 1b\n  });\n\n  it('falls back to the label prefix for Fase 1a nodes (no typeId)', () => {\n    expect(nodeTypeId(mkNode({ label: 'image-generator #2' }))).toBe('image-generator');\n    expect(classifyNode(mkNode({ label: 'image-generator #2' }))).toBe('executable');\n    expect(classifyNode(mkNode({ label: 'text #1' }))).toBe('passive');\n    expect(classifyNode(mkNode({ label: 'Meu n\u00f3 renomeado' }))).toBe('unsupported');\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('resolveSpacesModel (S8)', () => {\n  it('maps known keys and falls back to auto', () => {\n    expect(resolveSpacesModel('auto')).toEqual({ provider: 'openrouter', model: 'google/gemini-2.5-flash-image' });\n    expect(resolveSpacesModel('flux.1.1')).toEqual({ provider: 'openrouter', model: 'black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro' });\n    expect(resolveSpacesModel('krea-2')).toEqual(resolveSpacesModel('auto'));\n    expect(resolveSpacesModel(undefined)).toEqual(resolveSpacesModel('auto'));\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('estimateExecutableCost (G7 \u2014 estimate = real charge)', () => {\n  it('prices image-generator by resolved map \u00d7 batch (S10)', () => {\n    expect(estimateExecutableCost(mkNode({ typeId: 'image-generator' }))).toBe(SPACES_EXECUTABLE_COSTS['auto']);\n    expect(estimateExecutableCost(mkNode({ typeId: 'image-generator', modelKey: 'flux.1.1' }))).toBe(12);\n    expect(estimateExecutableCost(mkNode({ typeId: 'image-generator', batch: 3 }))).toBe(30);\n    // unknown model key estimates as auto (executor resolves it as auto \u2014 no estimate/charge gap)\n    expect(estimateExecutableCost(mkNode({ typeId: 'image-generator', modelKey: 'krea-2' }))).toBe(10);\n  });\n\n  it('prices prompt-generator by level (mirrors magic_prompt referenceImages: [] })).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('rowToGeneration (C9 row\u2192card mapper)', () => {\n  it('maps snake_case row to the zod card shape with previews', () => {\n    const {
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts",
  "old_string": "function spacesJson(status: number, payload: Record<string, unknown>, corsHeaders: Record<string, string>) {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), {\n    status, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n  });\n}",
  "new_string": "function spacesJson(status: number, payload: Record<string, unknown>, corsHeaders: Record<string, string>) {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), {\n    status, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n  });\n}\n\n/** finalize_space_generation with ONE retry (security-review MEDIUM 2026-07-02: a transient RPC blip\n *  must not silently strand the charge). Returns the finalize error (or null) so callers report the\n *  ledger TRUTH \u2014 never claim a refund that didn't commit. */\nasync function finalizeWithRetry(\n  admin: any,\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n): Promise<{ data: any; error: any }> {\n  let { data, error } = await admin.rpc(\"finalize_space_generation\", args);\n  if (error) {\n    console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] finalize failed, retrying once:\", error);\n    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));\n    ({ data, error } = await admin.rpc(\"finalize_space_generation\", args));\n    if (error) console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] finalize retry failed:\", error);\n  }\n  return { data, error };\n}\n\n/** Last-resort materialization when finalize(done) fails twice AFTER value was delivered\n *  (security-review LOW: a delivered-but-stuck 'running' row must not look sweep-refundable \u2014\n *  the sweep only refunds rows with NO delivered value). Money untouched (refund stays 0). */\nasync function materializeDoneFallback(\n  admin: any,\n  nodeRunId: string,\n  patch: Record<string, unknown>,\n) {\n  try {\n    const { error } = await admin\n      .from(\"generations\")\n      .update({ status: \"done\", ...patch })\n      .eq(\"node_run_id\", nodeRunId)\n      .in(\"status\", [\"pending\", \"running\"]);\n    if (error) console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] done-fallback update failed:\", error);\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"[canvas-execute:spaces] done-fallback update threw:\", e);\n  }\n}"
}
```

{

  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# self-heal-spaces.sh \u2014 Spaces Fase 1b stuck-run reconciliation (SOL-SPACES-002 / FMEA-SPACES-009).\n# SOP: docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md (Recovery path). ON-DEMAND \u2014 no cron (BoK 07:182).\n#\n# A generation stuck 'pending'/'running' for >30 min means the edge fn died between begin (debit)\n# and finalize. Rule: mark 'error' + FULL idempotent refund via finalize_space_generation \u2014 NEVER\n# re-charge. Safety guard (security-review LOW 2026-07-02): only rows with NO delivered value\n# (result IS NULL AND asset_id IS NULL) are refunded \u2014 a delivered-but-finalize-failed row is\n# materialized by the edge fn's done-fallback and must never be sweep-refunded (value stays paid).\n#\n# Usage: bash scripts/self-heal-spaces.sh [--dry-run]\nset -euo pipefail\ncd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/..\"\n\n# shellcheck disable=SC1091\nsource .env 2>/dev/null || true\n: \"${SB_SECRET_KEY:?SB_SECRET_KEY missing \u2014 source .env first}\"\nBASE=\"https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co\"\nAUTH=(-H \"apikey: ${SB_SECRET_KEY}\" -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${SB_SECRET_KEY}\")\n\nDRY=0\n[[ \"${1:-}\" == \"--dry-run\" ]] && DRY=1\n\nCUTOFF=$(date -u -d '30 minutes ago' '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')\necho \"[self-heal-spaces] scanning generations stuck before ${CUTOFF} (no delivered value)...\"\n\nROWS=$(curl -sf \"${BASE}/rest/v1/generations?select=node_run_id,mco_charged,updated_at&status=in.(pending,running)&updated_at=lt.${CUTOFF}&result=is.null&asset_id=is.null\" \"${AUTH[@]}\")\nCOUNT=$(echo \"${ROWS}\" | python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(len(json.load(sys.stdin)))')\necho \"[self-heal-spaces] ${COUNT} stuck row(s) found\"\n[[ \"${COUNT}\" == \"0\" ]] && exit 0\n\necho \"${ROWS}\" | python3 -c '\nimport json, sys\nfor r in json.load(sys.stdin):\n    print(f\"{r[\\\"node_run_id\\\"]} {r[\\\"mco_charged\\\"]} {r[\\\"updated_at\\\"]}\")' | while read -r RUN_ID CHARGED UPDATED; do\n  if [[ \"${DRY}\" == \"1\" ]]; then\n    echo \"[dry-run] would refund ${CHARGED} mco for ${RUN_ID} (stuck since ${UPDATED})\"\n    continue\n  fi\n  RESULT=$(curl -sf -X POST \"${BASE}/rest/v1/rpc/finalize_space_generation\" \\\n    \"${AUTH[@]}\" -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n    -d \"{\\\"p_node_run_id\\\":\\\"${RUN_ID}\\\",\\\"p_status\\\":\\\"error\\\",\\\"p_refund\\\":${CHARGED}}\")\n  echo \"[self-heal-spaces] ${RUN_ID} \u2192 ${RESULT}\"\ndone\n\necho \"[self-heal-spaces] done\"\n"
}

> scripts/qa/lib/qa-throwaway.ts

> scripts/qa/smoke-economic-freeze.ts
> ---
> #!/usr/bin/env bun
> /**
>  * Mint a REAL user access_token (JWT) for authenticated edge-fn calls in QA — without a browser or raw password.
>  * Admin generateLink (magiclink) → verifyOtp(token_hash) → session.access_token. Per-user, reproducible.
>  * Pattern extends gen-magiclink.ts (v6.20.4). The token is one-time and ~1h-lived; mint right before use.
>  *
>  * Usage: bun run scripts/qa/gen-user-jwt.ts <email>
>  *   → prints the access_token on stdout (nothing else), or an error on stderr + exit 1.
>  *   e.g. TOKEN=$(bun run scripts/qa/gen-user-jwt.ts gabrielcall@gmail.com)
>  */
> import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
> 
> const URL = process.env.SUPABASE_URL ?? process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL!;
> const SVC = (process.env.SB_SECRET_KEY ?? process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)!;
> const ANON = (process.env.SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? process.env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY)!;
> const email = process.argv[2];
> if (!URL || !SVC || !ANON || !email) {
>   console.error('Usage: gen-user-jwt.ts <email>  (needs SUPABASE_URL + SB_SECRET_KEY + SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY)');
>   process.exit(1);
> }
> 
> const admin = createClient(URL, SVC, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
> const anon = createClient(URL, ANON, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
> 
> const { data, error } = await admin.auth.admin.generateLink({ type: 'magiclink', email });
> if (error || !data.properties?.hashed_token) {
>   console.error('generateLink error:', error?.message ?? 'no hashed_token');
>   process.exit(1);
> }
> const tokenHash = data.properties.hashed_token;
> 
> let accessToken: string | null = null;
> for (const type of ['magiclink', 'email'] as const) {
>   const { data: sess, error: vErr } = await anon.auth.verifyOtp({ token_hash: tokenHash, type });
>   if (!vErr && sess?.session?.access_token) { accessToken = sess.session.access_token; break; }
> }
> if (!accessToken) { console.error('verifyOtp failed for both magiclink/email types'); process.exit(1); }
> console.log(accessToken);
> ```

> **Tool Result (Bash)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> /**
>  * QA throwaway-user lifecycle — single source of truth for smoke/probe test accounts.
>  *
>  * Why this exists: every smoke minted an anonymous `*-<stamp>@example.com` user with a fire-and-forget
>  * cleanup (`deleteUser(uid).then(()=>{}, ()=>{})`). When the process was killed before `finally`, or the
>  * delete errored, the account leaked — surfacing in /dashboard/admin as a nameless `viewer` (trialing,
>  * balance 0) that looked like a real signup (2026-06-28: `lf16-smoke-...@example.com` from smoke-longform-16x9).
>  *
>  * Two-layer defense:
>  *   1. IDENTIFY — every throwaway carries `user_metadata.full_name = 'SMOKETEST'`, which the
>  *      `handle_new_user` trigger copies into `profiles.full_name` → the admin "Nome" column shows SMOKETEST.
>  *      Also tags `qa_throwaway: true` + the smoke slug for programmatic sweeps.
>  *   2. AUTO-CLEAN — `deleteThrowawayUser` is awaited and logs on failure (no silent swallow), and
>  *      `sweepThrowawayUsers` removes any orphan that slipped past a smoke's `finally`. The standalone
>  *      `scripts/qa/sweep-smoke-users.ts` (and its cron) is the backstop that self-heals leaks.
>  *
>  * Safety invariant: NOTHING outside the `@example.com` domain (or explicit `qa_throwaway` metadata) is ever
>  * touched. Real users (e.g. gabrielcall@gmail.com) can never match.
>  */
> import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
> 
> export const QA_THROWAWAY_NAME = 'SMOKETEST';
> export const QA_THROWAWAY_DOMAIN = '@example.com';
> 
> export interface ThrowawayUser {
>   uid: string;
>   email: string;
>   password: string;
> }
> 
> /** Deterministic, collision-resistant throwaway email bound to a smoke slug. */
> export function qaThrowawayEmail(prefix: string): string {
>   return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}${QA_THROWAWAY_DOMAIN}`;
> }
> 
> /**
>  * Create a confirmed, SMOKETEST-tagged throwaway user. Returns the password too, since several smokes
>  * sign in with it to mint a user JWT. Throws on failure (smokes already wrap setup in try/catch).
>  */
> export async function createThrowawayUser(
>   admin: SupabaseClient,
>   prefix: string,
>   extraMetadata: Record<string, unknown> = {},
> ): Promise<ThrowawayUser> {
>   const email = qaThrowawayEmail(prefix);
>   const password = `Aa1!${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
>   const { data, error } = await admin.auth.admin.createUser({
>     email,
>     password,
>     email_confirm: true,
>     user_metadata: { full_name: QA_THROWAWAY_NAME, qa_throwaway: true, smoke: prefix, ...extraMetadata },
>   });
>   if (error || !data?.user) throw new Error(`createThrowawayUser(${prefix}): ${error?.message ?? 'no user'}`);
>   return { uid: data.user.id, email, password };
> }
> 
> /**
>  * Delete a throwaway user, awaited, with a visible warning on failure (replaces the fire-and-forget pattern).
>  * Returns true on success. Never throws — safe to call inside a `finally`.
>  */
> export async function deleteThrowawayUser(admin: SupabaseClient, uid: string | null | undefined): Promise<boolean> {
>   if (!uid) return false;
>   const { error } = await admin.auth.admin.deleteUser(uid);
>   if (error) {
>     console.warn(`⚠️  deleteThrowawayUser(${uid}) failed: ${error.message} — run scripts/qa/sweep-smoke-users.ts to reap orphans`);
>     return false;
>   }
>   return true;
> }
> ```

await createThrowawayUser(admin, 'spaces1b-b');\n  uidA = a.uid; uidB = b.uid;\n  const anonA = createClient(URL, ANON, { auth: { persistSession: false } });\n  const anonB = createClient(URL, ANON, { auth: { persistSession: false } });\n  const { data: sessA } = await anonA.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: a.email, password: a.password });\n  const { data: sessB } = await throwaways');\n\n  const { data: spaceA } = await admin.from('spaces')\n    .insert({ owner_id: a.uid, title: 'smoke-1b-A' }).select('id').single();\n  const { data: spaceB } = await p_amount: startBal, p_action: 'qa_drain', p_context: { smoke: 'spaces1b' } });\n  }\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T1 \u2014 401 without JWT \u2500\u2500\n  const t1 = await invokeFn(null, spacePayload(spaceA.id));\n  gate('T1 401 sem JWT', t1.status === 401, `status=${t1.status}`);\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T2 \u2014 422 unsupported node type (before any debit) \u2500\u2500\n  const t2 = await invokeFn(jwtA, spacePayload(spaceA.id, { node_type: 'image_to_video', model: 'dop-standard', provider: 'higgsfield' }));\n  gate('T2 422 node-type fora do slice', t2.status === 422 && t2.json.error === 'spaces_node_type_unsupported', `status=${t2.status} error=${t2.json.error}`);\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T3 \u2014 422 unsupported model pair \u2500\u2500\n  const t3 = await invokeFn(jwtA, spacePayload(spaceA.id, { provider: 'openai', model: 'dall-e-3' }));\n  gate('T3 422 modelo fora do allowlist', t3.status === 422 && t3.json.error === 'spaces_model_unsupported', `status=${t3.status} error=${t3.json.error}`);\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T4 \u2014 404 foreign/absent space (FR-SPACES-007: tenant \u2192 not found) \u2500\u2500\n  const t4a = await invokeFn(jwtA, spacePayload(crypto.randomUUID()));\n  const t4b = await invokeFn(jwtA, spacePayload(spaceB.id));\n  gate('T4 404 space alheio/inexistente', t4a.status === 404 && t4b.status === 404, `random=${t4a.status} foreign=${t4b.status}`);\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T5 \u2014 402 insufficient balance (shared pre-check; zero generations rows) \u2500\u2500\n  const t5 = await invokeFn(jwtA, spacePayload(spaceA.id));\n  const { count: rowsAfter402 } = await admin.from('generations').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('owner_id', a.uid);\n  gate('T5 402 saldo + zero rows', t5.status === 402 && (rowsAfter402 ?? 0) === 0, `status=${t5.status} rows=${rowsAfter402}`);\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T6 \u2014 begin/finalize atomics (direct service-role RPC; no provider, no edge fn needed) \u2500\u2500\n  await admin.rpc('add_mco_coins', { p_user_id: a.uid, p_amount: 50 });\n  const run1 = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const { data: beg1, error: begErr1 } = await admin.rpc('begin_space_generation', {\n    p_owner_id: a.uid, p_space_id: spaceA.id, p_node_id: 'n1', p_node_run_id: run1,\n    p_model_key: 'qa/test', p_output_type: 'image', p_amount: 10, p_context: { smoke: 'spaces1b' },\n  });\n  const balAfterBegin = await balance(a.uid);\n  gate('T6a begin ledger-first (row+d\u00e9bito)', !begErr1 && beg1?.ok === true && beg1?.duplicate === false && balAfterBegin === 40,\n    `bal=${balAfterBegin} beg=${JSON.stringify(beg1)}`);\n\n  const { data: beg1dup } = await admin.rpc('begin_space_generation', {\n    p_owner_id: a.uid, p_space_id: spaceA.id, p_node_id: 'n1', p_node_run_id: run1,\n    p_model_key: 'qa/test', p_output_type: 'image', p_amount: 10, p_context: {},\n  });\n  gate('T6b duplicata idempotente (zero 2\u00ba d\u00e9bito)', beg1dup?.duplicate === true && (await balance(a.uid)) === 40,\n    `dup=${JSON.stringify(beg1dup)} bal=${await balance(a.uid)}`);\n\n  const run2 = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const { error: begErr2 } = await admin.rpc('begin_space_generation', {\n    p_owner_id: a.uid, p_space_id: spaceA.id, p_node_id: 'n1', p_node_run_id: run2,\n    p_model_key: 'qa/test', p_output_type: 'image', p_amount: 999, p_context: {},\n  });\n  const { count: run2rows } = await admin.from('generations').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('node_run_id', run2);\n  gate('T6c saldo insuficiente \u2192 rollback total (zero row)', !!begErr2 && (run2rows ?? 0) === 0 && (await balance(a.uid)) === 40,\n    `err=${begErr2?.message?.slice(0, 40)} rows=${run2rows}`);\n\n  const { data: fin1 } = await admin.rpc('finalize_space_generation', { p_node_run_id: run1, p_status: 'error', p_refund: 10 });\n  const balAfterRefund = await balance(a.uid);\n  const { data: refundRows } = await admin.from('mcoin_transactions').select('amount')\n    .eq('user_id', a.uid).eq('action', 'spaces.node.refund');\n  gate('T6d finalize(error) \u2192 refund total + row sim\u00e9trica', fin1?.finalized === true && fin1?.refunded === 10 && balAfterRefund === 50 && (refundRows ?? []).length === 1,\n    `fin=${JSON.stringify(fin1)} bal=${balAfterRefund}`);\n\n  const { data: fin1again } = await admin.rpc('finalize_space_generation', { p_node_run_id: run1, p_status: 'error', p_refund: 10 });\n  gate('T6e finalize 2\u00d7 \u2192 no-op (sem double-refund)', fin1again?.finalized === false && (await balance(a.uid)) === 50,\n    `fin2=${JSON.stringify(fin1again)}`);\n\n  const run3 = crypto.randomUUID();\n  await admin.rpc('begin_space_generation', {\n    p_owner_id: a.uid, p_space_id: spaceA.id, p_node_id: 'n2', p_node_run_id: run3,\n    p_model_key: 'qa/test', p_output_type: 'text', p_amount: 5, p_context: {},\n  });\n  const { data: fin3 } = await admin.rpc('finalize_space_generation', { p_node_run_id: run3, p_status: 'error', p_refund: 999 });\n  gate('T6f refund clampado ao cobrado (anti-mint)', fin3?.finalized === true && Number(fin3?.refunded) === 5 && (await balance(a.uid)) === 50,\n    `fin=${JSON.stringify(fin3)} bal=${await balance(a.uid)}`);\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T7 \u2014 RLS: SELECT-own \u00b7 no client INSERT \u00b7 no client DELETE \u2500\u2500\n  const { data: ownRows } = await anonA.from('generations').select('id').eq('owner_id', a.uid);\n  const { data: crossRows } = await anonB.from('generations').select('id').eq('owner_id', a.uid);\n  const { error: insErr } = await anonA.from('generations').insert({\n    owner_id: a.uid, space_id: spaceA.id, node_id: 'x', node_run_id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n    output_type: 'image', model_key: 'hack', mco_charged: 0,\n  } as never);\n  await anonA.from('generations').delete().eq('owner_id', a.uid);\n  const { count: afterDel } = await admin.from('generations').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('owner_id', a.uid);\n  gate('T7 RLS own-only + sem INSERT/DELETE client', (ownRows ?? []).length === 2 && (crossRows ?? []).length === 0 && !!insErr && (afterDel ?? 0) === 2,\n    `own=${ownRows?.length} cross=${crossRows?.length} ins=${insErr ? 'blocked' : 'ALLOWED!'} rows=${afterDel}`);\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 T8 \u2014 RPC privilege: authenticated caller \u2192 42501 \u2500\u2500\n  const { error: privErr } = await anonA.rpc('begin_space_generation', {\n    p_owner_id: a.uid, p_space_id: spaceA.id, p_node_id: 'n1', p_node_run_id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n    p_model_key: 'qa/test', p_output_type: 'image', p_amount: 1, p_context: {},\n  } as never);\n  gate('T8 RPC negada a authenticated', !!privErr && /permission denied|42501/i.test(privErr.message), privErr?.message?.slice(0, 60));\n\n} catch (e) {\n  fail++;\n  console.error('\ud83d\udca5 smoke aborted:', (e as Error).message);\n} finally {\n  await deleteThrowawayUser(admin, uidA);\n  await deleteThrowawayUser(admin, uidB);\n}\n\nconsole.log(`\\n${fail === 0 ? '\u2705' : '\u274c'} smoke-spaces-execution: ${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);\nprocess.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/test/spaces-mirror-parity.test.ts",
  "content": "// spaces-mirror-parity.test.ts \u2014 SOL-SPACES-001 mechanical mirror-parity gate (FMEA-SPACES-004).\n// The client estimate map (SPACES_EXECUTABLE_COSTS / SPACES_MODEL_MAP) MUST match the server\n// authority (CREDIT_COSTS + SPACES_IMAGE_MODELS in canvas-execute). This test parses the deployed\n// edge-fn SOURCE so a drift on either side fails CI before it can lie to the user (gate G7).\nimport { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'node:path';\nimport { SPACES_MODEL_MAP, SPACES_EXECUTABLE_COSTS } from '@/lib/spaces-execution';\n\nconst fnSource = readFileSync(\n  resolve(__dirname, '../../supabase/functions/canvas-execute/index.ts'),\n  'utf-8',\n);\n\n/** Parse `\"key\": <number>,` entries of the CREDIT_COSTS literal. */\nfunction parseCreditCosts(src: string): Record<string, number> {\n  const block = src.match(/const CREDIT_COSTS[^=]*=\\s*{([\\s\\S]*?)};/)?.[1] ?? '';\n  const costs: Record<string, number> = {};\n  for (const m of block.matchAll(/\"([^\"]+)\":\\s*(\\d+)/g)) costs[m[1]] = Number(m[2]);\n  return costs;\n}\n\n/** Parse the SPACES_IMAGE_MODELS allowlist entries. */\nfunction parseSpacesAllowlist(src: string): string[] {\n  const block = src.match(/const SPACES_IMAGE_MODELS = new Set\\(\\[([\\s\\S]*?)\\]\\)/)?.[1] ?? '';\n  return [...block.matchAll(/\"([^\"]+)\"/g)].map((m) => m[1]);\n}\n\ndescribe('mirror parity \u2014 client estimate vs server authority (SOL-SPACES-001)', () => {\n  const serverCosts = parseCreditCosts(fnSource);\n  const serverAllowlist = parseSpacesAllowlist(fnSource);\n\n  it('parses the server maps (guard against refactors breaking the parser)', () => {\n    expect(Object.keys(serverCosts).length).toBeGreaterThan(10);\n    expect(serverAllowlist.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  });\n\n  it('every client model resolves to a server-priced pair with the SAME cost', () => {\n    for (const [spacesKey, { provider, model }] of Object.entries(SPACES_MODEL_MAP)) {\n      const pair = `${provider}/${model}`;\n      expect(serverCosts[pair], `server CREDIT_COSTS missing ${pair}`).toBeDefined();\n      expect(SPACES_EXECUTABLE_COSTS[spacesKey], `client cost missing for ${spacesKey}`)\n        .toBe(serverCosts[pair]);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('client map and server allowlist are the SAME set (no orphan on either side)', () => {\n    const clientPairs = Object.values(SPACES_MODEL_MAP).map((m) => `${m.provider}/${m.model}`).sort();\n    expect([...new Set(clientPairs)]).toEqual([...serverAllowlist].sort());\n  });\n});\n"
}

## PHASE 1 — SECURITY AUDIT

Run these checks and report findings. **Block the seal if any CRITICAL finding exists.**

> ⚠️ **SCOPE NOTE:** This phase scans changes that exist NOW (before Phase 5 writes HANDOFF.md).

```bash
# 1a. Secret leak scan — check staged + working tree for hardcoded credentials
git diff HEAD | grep -E "(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{20,}|STRIPE_|SECRET_KEY|ACCESS_TOKEN|api_key\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,})" \
  | grep -v "example\|placeholder\|<.*>\|your-key\|YOUR_" | head -20

# 1b. Edge function JWT enforcement — every user-facing function must verify JWT
grep -rL "Authorization\|jwt\|JWT\|service_role" supabase/functions/*/index.ts \
  | grep -v "get-infra-status\|watchdog" || echo "ALL FUNCTIONS: JWT enforced ✅"

# 1c. Client-side coin deduction guard — must NEVER update mco_balance directly from client
grep -rn "mco_balance.*update\|UPDATE.*mco_balance" src/ \
  | grep -v "settings\|top.up\|topup\|SettingsPage" | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1d. RLS bypass risk — check for supabase.rpc calls without auth context
grep -rn "service_role\|bypass.*rls\|rls.*bypass" src/ | head -10 || echo "NO VIOLATIONS ✅"

# 1e. Sensitive data in console.log
grep -rn "console\.log.*token\|console\.log.*key\|console\.log.*secret\|console\.log.*password" src/ \
  | head -10 || echo "NO SENSITIVE LOGS ✅"
```

**Security verdict:** list each check as ✅ PASS / ⚠️ WARN / 🔴 BLOCK.
Only proceed if no 🔴 findings.

### DOCUMENTATION CREDENTIAL RULE (invariant — never violate)

**When writing HANDOFF.md, README.md, or any committed documentation:**

- ✅ DO: describe WHERE a credential is stored → `VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY` is set in `.env` (local) and `GEMINI_API_KEY` in the Supabase vault
- ✅ DO: reference digest/fingerprint if useful → `GEMINI_API_KEY (digest 0fe0e159...)`
- 🔴 NEVER: include the actual credential value → `AIzaSyBv...`, `sk-...`, `eyJ...`

> **Rationale:** Phase 1 scans changes that predate the HANDOFF.md write. Any credential embedded
> in HANDOFF.md during Phase 5 escapes Phase 1 entirely and goes directly to the remote.
> This rule is the primary prevention layer; Phase 5b is the detection backstop.

## PHASE 6 — BoK SEAL STATUS (mandatory before push)

**Verify any significant module work in this session has a sealed BoK suite per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.**

```bash
# 6-1. List BoK suites present + check completeness
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] \
    && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete (9 docs)" \
    || echo "⚠️ $slug — incomplete (missing: ${missing[*]})"
done

# 6-2. Check session touched code in new src/pages/ or new supabase/functions/ that lacks BoK
session_new_modules=$(git diff --name-only HEAD~10..HEAD 2>/dev/null | grep -E "^(src/pages/|supabase/functions/|src/components/[A-Z])" | awk -F/ '{print "/"}' | sort -u)
echo "Session touched modules: $session_new_modules"

# 6-3. Mesh seal nodes for sealed BoK suites
source .env 2>/dev/null
if [ -n "$SB_SECRET_KEY" ]; then
  curl -s "$SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?node_type=eq.documentation_suite&select=id,name,stability_score,created_at" \
    -H "apikey: $SB_SECRET_KEY" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $SB_SECRET_KEY" \
    | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); [print(f\"  🌐 {n['name']} ({n['stability_score']})\") for n in d]" 2>/dev/null \
    || echo "  ⚠️ Could not query seal nodes"
fi
```

**Gate verdict:**
- ✅ All session modules have sealed BoK → proceed to Phase 7 push.
- ⚠️ Module work without BoK (e.g. `feat:` commit creating new page/edge fn without `docs/bok/<slug>/`)
  → 🔴 **BLOCK seal**. Run `/bok-scribe <module>` first OR add module to exemption list (typo/dep bump/single-file patch).

Report BoK status in final seal output (Phase 8).

---

## PHASE 7 — PUSH

```bash
git push origin main
```

Report: branch, number of commits pushed, remote URL.

If push fails (non-fast-forward): run `git pull --rebase origin main` first, then push again. Never force-push main.

---

## PHASE 7b — SPRINT & CONNECTIONS HYGIENE

### Sprint priorities update

Review `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` and update:
1. Check off completed items in "Skills a criar ou evoluir" and "Conexões a implementar"
2. Add new gaps discovered this session under "Top 3 Gap Closures" if applicable
3. Update the 4Cs Audit Snapshot scores if `/audit` was run this session
4. Append to the Retrospective section if this was the last session of the sprint

Commit if changed:
```
chore(sprint): update sprint-priorities — <one-line progress note>
```

### Scratch cleanup

```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null
```

If `scratch/` has `.ts` diagnostic scripts, move them to `.claude/scripts/db/`:
```bash
mkdir -p .claude/scripts/db
mv scratch/*.ts .claude/scripts/db/ 2>/dev/null && echo "Moved ✅" || echo "Nothing to move"
```

Commit if files were moved:
```
chore(scripts): migrate scratch diagnostics to .claude/scripts/db/
```

### MCP documentation check

```bash
cat .mcp.json
```

For each MCP server listed, verify it has an entry in `.claude/references/` or is documented in CLAUDE.md. If a new MCP was added this session and has no documentation, add a one-liner to the relevant reference file.

---

## PHASE 8 — FINAL REPORT

Print seal summary in **Portuguese (Brasil)**:

```
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  HANDOFF SEALED — <PHASE NAME> (<date>)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

🔐 SEGURANÇA
  <security check results — each as ✅ / ⚠️ / 🔴>

📦 COMMITS (<N> total)
  <hash> — <message>
  ...

⬡ KNOWLEDGE MESH
  Milestone node: <node id> — <name>
  Embedding: ✅ / ⚠️

📖 README.md
  Versão: <old> → <new>
  Badge neural_mesh: <old> → <new> nós
  Seção adicionada: <section name>

🧠 HANDOFF.md
  Fase selada: <phase name>
  Seções atualizadas: Task State, Record, Pending Actions, GraphRAG State

📋 PROVA MATERIAL (Fase 5c)
  Veredito: <SEAL ALLOWED / SEAL BLOCKED>
  Claims: <N> verificadas · <M> skipped · <K> falhas

🚀 PUSH
  Branch: main → origin/main
  Commits enviados: <N>

📌 SPRINT
  sprint-priorities.md: <atualizado ✅ / sem mudanças>
  scratch/ cleanup: <arquivos movidos ✅ / limpo ✅>
  MCP docs: <ok ✅ / pendência: X>

⏭  PRÓXIMOS PASSOS
  1. <top pending action>
  2. <second pending action>
  3. <third pending action>

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  Assinado: Claude Code — Braço de Engenharia do AIOS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```

**Invariants — never skip:**
- Security audit must run and report before any commit (Phase 1)
- **Phase 3 (milestone node) must run BEFORE Phase 4 (README badge query)** — ensures count includes the handoff node
- **Phase 5b secret scan must run on HANDOFF.md before its commit** — no exceptions
- **Phase 5c material proof audit must run and return `SEAL ALLOWED` before push** — a contradicted claim blocks the seal
- **Documentation must NEVER contain actual credential values** — only describe where they live
- README.md badge counts must reflect real DB state (query live, after milestone insert)
- HANDOFF.md must be updated even if nothing changed (session note)
- Push must happen — remote must always mirror local main
- Knowledge Mesh milestone must be attempted (failures are logged, not blocking)
- git pre-commit hook (`.git/hooks/pre-commit`) is the last barrier — do not bypass with `--no-verify`

# /loop — schedule a recurring or self-paced prompt

Parse the input below into `[interval] <prompt…>` and schedule it.

## Parsing (in priority order)

1. **Leading token**: if the first whitespace-delimited token matches `^\d+[smhd]$` (e.g. `5m`, `2h`), that's the interval; the rest is the prompt.
2. **Trailing "every" clause**: otherwise, if the input ends with `every <N><unit>` or `every <N> <unit-word>` (e.g. `every 20m`, `every 5 minutes`, `every 2 hours`), extract that as the interval and strip it from the prompt. Only match when what follows "every" is a time expression — `check every PR` has no interval.
3. **No interval**: otherwise, the entire input is the prompt and you'll self-pace dynamically (see "Dynamic mode" below).

If the resulting prompt is empty, show usage `/loop [interval] <prompt>` and stop.

Examples:
- `5m /babysit-prs` → interval `5m`, prompt `/babysit-prs` (rule 1)
- `check the deploy every 20m` → interval `20m`, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 2)
- `run tests every 5 minutes` → interval `5m`, prompt `run tests` (rule 2)
- `check the deploy` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check the deploy` (rule 3)
- `check every PR` → no interval → dynamic mode, prompt `check every PR` (rule 3 — "every" not followed by time)
- `5m` → empty prompt → show usage

## Offer cloud first

Before any scheduling step, check whether EITHER is true:
- the parsed interval (rule 1 or 2) is **≥60 minutes**, or
- regardless of which rule matched, the original input uses daily phrasing ("every morning", "daily", "every day", "each night", "every weekday")

If either is true, call AskUserQuestion first:
- `question`: "This loop stops when you close this session. Set it up as a cloud schedule instead so it keeps running?"
- `header`: "Schedule"
- `options`: `[{label: "Cloud schedule (recommended)", description: "Runs in Anthropic's cloud even after you close this session"}, {label: "This session only", description: "Runs in this terminal until you exit"}]`

If they pick **Cloud schedule**: do NOT call CronCreate. Invoke the `schedule` skill directly via the Skill tool with `args` set to their original input verbatim (e.g. `Skill({skill: "schedule", args: "every morning tell me a joke"})`), then follow that skill's instructions to completion. Do NOT tell the user to run /schedule themselves. **Then stop — do not continue to any section below** (no CronCreate, no ScheduleWakeup, no "execute the prompt now").
If they pick **This session only**:
- If the trigger was a parsed ≥60-minute interval (rule 1 or 2): continue below with that interval.
- If the trigger was daily phrasing only (rule 3, no parsed interval): do NOT call CronCreate. Explain that a daily-cadence loop won't fire before this session closes, so there's nothing useful to schedule locally — suggest they either pick Cloud schedule, or re-run `/loop` with an explicit shorter interval (e.g. `/loop 1h <prompt>`) if they want a session loop. Then stop.
If neither trigger condition was met: continue below.

## Fixed-interval mode (rules 1 and 2)

Convert the interval to a cron expression:

| Interval pattern      | Cron expression     | Notes                                    |
|-----------------------|---------------------|------------------------------------------|
| `Nm` where N ≤ 59   | `*/N * * * *`     | every N minutes                          |
| `Nm` where N ≥ 60   | `0 */H * * *`     | round to hours (H = N/60, must divide 24)|
| `Nh` where N ≤ 23   | `0 */N * * *`     | every N hours                            |
| `Nd`                | `0 0 */N * *`     | every N days at midnight local           |
| `Ns`                | treat as `ceil(N/60)m` | cron minimum granularity is 1 minute  |

**If the interval doesn't cleanly divide its unit** (e.g. `7m` → `*/7 * * * *` gives uneven gaps at :56→:00; `90m` → 1.5h which cron can't express), pick the nearest clean interval and tell the user what you rounded to before scheduling.

Then:
1. Call CronCreate with: `cron` (the expression above), `prompt` (the parsed prompt verbatim), `recurring: true`.
2. Briefly confirm: what's scheduled, the cron expression, the human-readable cadence, that recurring tasks auto-expire after 7 days, and that the user can cancel sooner with CronDelete (include the job ID). Only if you did NOT show the cloud-offer AskUserQuestion above (i.e., neither trigger condition applied), end the confirmation with this exact line on its own, italicized: `_Runs until you close this session · For durable cloud-based loops, use /schedule_`. If the user already answered that question, omit this line.
3. **Then immediately execute the parsed prompt now** — don't wait for the first cron fire. If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.

## Dynamic mode (rule 3 — no interval)

The user wants you to self-pace. Decide what makes the next iteration worth running — a passage of time, or an observable event.

1. **Run the parsed prompt now.** If it's a slash command, invoke it via the Skill tool; otherwise act on it directly.
2. **If the next run is gated on an event** (CI finishing, a log line matching, a file changing, a PR comment) and no Monitor is already running for it: arm one now with `persistent: true`. Its events arrive as `<task-notification>` messages and wake this loop immediately — you do not wait for the ScheduleWakeup deadline. Arm once; on later iterations call TaskList first and skip this step if a monitor is already running.
3. **Briefly confirm**: that you're self-pacing, whether a Monitor is the primary wake signal, that you ran the task now, and what fallback delay you're about to pick. Write this as text *before* calling ScheduleWakeup — the turn ends as soon as that tool returns.
4. **Then, as the last action of this turn, call ScheduleWakeup** with:
   - `delaySeconds`: with a Monitor armed this is the **fallback heartbeat** — how long to wait if no event fires (lean 1200–1800s; idle ticks past the 5-minute cache window are pure overhead). Without a Monitor this is the cadence — pick based on what you observed. Read the tool's own description for cache-aware delay guidance.
   - `reason`: one short sentence on why you picked that delay.
   - `prompt`: the full original /loop input verbatim, prefixed with `/loop ` so the next firing re-enters this skill and continues the loop. For example, if the user typed `/loop check the deploy`, pass `/loop check the deploy` as the prompt.
5. **If you were woken by a `<task-notification>`** rather than this prompt: handle the event in the context of the loop task, then call ScheduleWakeup again with the same `prompt` and the same 1200–1800s `delaySeconds` from step 4 — the Monitor remains the wake signal; this only resets the safety net.
6. **To stop the loop**, omit the ScheduleWakeup call and TaskStop any Monitor you armed (use TaskList to find the task ID if it is no longer in context). Before you stop, send a one-line outcome via PushNotification — the user may be away and waiting to hear it's done. Skip this if you're stopping because the user just told you to; they're already here.
